### FILE: reference/rx_afe/work/netlist.py
<!-- sha256=c08c72b5de8552fa5071e1c7111f7777376926bf1f9c94ab7a025faa27fbbe45 newline=1 -->
````python
"""HSPICE / Virtuoso-style netlist parser + flattener (no name-based rules)."""
import re

SI = {'f': 1e-15, 'p': 1e-12, 'n': 1e-9, 'u': 1e-6, 'm': 1e-3, 'k': 1e3,
      'meg': 1e6, 'g': 1e9, 't': 1e12}


def num(s):
    s = s.strip().lower()
    m = re.match(r'^([-+]?[0-9.]+(?:e[-+]?\d+)?)(meg|[fpnumkgt])?', s)
    if not m:
        return None
    v = float(m.group(1))
    if m.group(2):
        v *= SI[m.group(2)]
    return v


def parse(path):
    raw = open(path).read().splitlines()
    lines = []
    for ln in raw:
        s = ln.strip()
        if not s or s.startswith('*'):
            continue
        if s.startswith('+'):
            lines[-1] += ' ' + s[1:].strip()
        else:
            lines.append(s)
    cells = {}
    order = []
    cur = None
    for s in lines:
        tok = s.split()
        t0 = tok[0].upper()
        if t0 == '.SUBCKT':
            cur = {'name': tok[1], 'ports': tok[2:], 'elems': []}
            cells[tok[1]] = cur
            order.append(tok[1])
        elif t0 == '.ENDS':
            cur = None
        elif t0.startswith('.'):
            continue
        elif cur is not None:
            name = tok[0]
            k = name[0].upper()
            if k == 'M':
                d, g, s_, b, model = tok[1:6]
                params = {}
                for p in tok[6:]:
                    if '=' in p:
                        a, v = p.split('=', 1)
                        params[a.lower()] = v
                mtype = 'p' if model.upper().startswith('P') else 'n'
                cur['elems'].append({'name': name, 'kind': 'mos', 'type': mtype, 'model': model,
                                     'terms': {'d': d, 'g': g, 's': s_, 'b': b}, 'params': params})
            elif k in 'RCL':
                n1, n2 = tok[1:3]
                val = tok[3] if len(tok) > 3 else ''
                cur['elems'].append({'name': name, 'kind': {'R': 'res', 'C': 'cap', 'L': 'ind'}[k],
                                     'terms': {'p': n1, 'n': n2}, 'value': val})
            elif k == 'X':
                # last token without '=' is the subckt name
                toks = [t for t in tok[1:] if '=' not in t]
                sub = toks[-1]
                nodes = toks[:-1]
                cur['elems'].append({'name': name, 'kind': 'inst', 'cell': sub, 'nodes': nodes})
    # resolve instance pin maps
    for c in cells.values():
        for e in c['elems']:
            if e['kind'] == 'inst':
                sub = cells[e['cell']]
                if len(sub['ports']) != len(e['nodes']):
                    raise ValueError('port count mismatch %s in %s' % (e['name'], c['name']))
                e['pins'] = dict(zip(sub['ports'], e['nodes']))
    return cells, order


def cell_nets(cell):
    nets = []
    seen = set()
    for p in cell['ports']:
        if p not in seen:
            nets.append(p); seen.add(p)
    for e in cell['elems']:
        vals = e['pins'].values() if e['kind'] == 'inst' else e['terms'].values()
        for n in vals:
            if n not in seen:
                nets.append(n); seen.add(n)
    return nets


def flatten(cells, top):
    """Return list of flat primitive devices. Each: path, cell, local, kind, type, terms(global), lterms(local), params/value, inst (instance path)."""
    devs = []

    def rec(cname, prefix, netmap):
        c = cells[cname]
        def g(n):
            if n in netmap:
                return netmap[n]
            return prefix + n
        for e in c['elems']:
            if e['kind'] == 'inst':
                sub = cells[e['cell']]
                nm = {p: g(n) for p, n in e['pins'].items()}
                rec(e['cell'], prefix + e['name'] + '/', nm)
            else:
                d = {'path': prefix + e['name'], 'cell': cname, 'local': e['name'], 'kind': e['kind'],
                     'inst': prefix, 'lterms': dict(e['terms']),
                     'terms': {k: g(v) for k, v in e['terms'].items()}}
                if e['kind'] == 'mos':
                    d['type'] = e['type']; d['params'] = e['params']; d['model'] = e['model']
                else:
                    d['value'] = e.get('value')
                devs.append(d)
    top_ports = cells[top]['ports']
    rec(top, '', {p: p for p in top_ports})
    return devs


def instance_tree(cells, top):
    """list of (inst_path, cell, netmap local->global)"""
    out = []

    def rec(cname, prefix, netmap):
        out.append((prefix, cname, dict(netmap)))
        c = cells[cname]
        def g(n):
            return netmap.get(n, prefix + n)
        for e in c['elems']:
            if e['kind'] == 'inst':
                nm = {p: g(n) for p, n in e['pins'].items()}
                # also add internal nets lazily: handled by g in child
                rec(e['cell'], prefix + e['name'] + '/', nm)
    rec(top, '', {p: p for p in cells[top]['ports']})
    # complete netmaps with internal nets
    res = []
    for prefix, cname, nm in out:
        full = {}
        for n in cell_nets(cells[cname]):
            full[n] = nm.get(n, prefix + n)
        res.append((prefix, cname, full))
    return res


def fmt_len(v):
    """format W/L string like '24u' '45n'"""
    return v
````

### FILE: reference/rx_afe/work/analyze.py
<!-- sha256=46eff9bb9907f5c3ad3f214df7a5a086ba8aae29d792d099e2d7e0cdcdbd35a9 newline=1 -->
````python
"""Step 1 (plan section 3): structural analysis.
device roles, signal tracing (data / clock), signal classes, important-control detection,
block roles, confidence, questions. Writes analysis.json, annotations.json, classification.md
Usage: python3 analyze.py <netlist> <top> <outdir> [hs_input ...]
"""
import sys, json, re, collections
from netlist import parse, flatten, instance_tree, cell_nets, num

NET_SEP = '/'


class Flat:
    def __init__(self, cells, top):
        self.cells = cells
        self.top = top
        self.devs = flatten(cells, top)
        self.by_net = collections.defaultdict(list)   # net -> [(dev, term)]
        for d in self.devs:
            for t, n in d['terms'].items():
                if d['kind'] == 'mos' and t == 'b':
                    continue
                self.by_net[n].append((d, t))
        self.find_rails()
        self.eff = {}
        for d in self.devs:
            if d['kind'] == 'mos':
                self.eff[d['path']] = self.eff_sd(d)

    # ---------- rails: structural (most common bulk of P / N devices) ----------
    def find_rails(self):
        pb = collections.Counter(d['terms']['b'] for d in self.devs if d['kind'] == 'mos' and d['type'] == 'p')
        nb = collections.Counter(d['terms']['b'] for d in self.devs if d['kind'] == 'mos' and d['type'] == 'n')
        self.supply = pb.most_common(1)[0][0]
        self.ground = nb.most_common(1)[0][0]
        self.rails = {self.supply, self.ground}

    def is_rail(self, n):
        return n in self.rails

    def dist_to_rail(self, start, mtype):
        rail = self.ground if mtype == 'n' else self.supply
        seen = {start: 0}
        q = collections.deque([start])
        while q:
            x = q.popleft()
            if x == rail:
                return seen[x]
            for d, t in self.by_net[x]:
                if d['kind'] == 'mos' and d['type'] == mtype and t in 'ds':
                    o = d['terms']['s' if t == 'd' else 'd']
                    if o not in seen:
                        seen[o] = seen[x] + 1
                        q.append(o)
        return 99

    def eff_sd(self, d):
        """effective (source, drain) by structure: source = terminal nearer own rail."""
        a, b = d['terms']['s'], d['terms']['d']
        own = self.ground if d['type'] == 'n' else self.supply
        other = self.supply if d['type'] == 'n' else self.ground
        if a == own: return a, b
        if b == own: return b, a
        if b == other: return a, b
        if a == other: return b, a
        da, db = self.dist_to_rail(a, d['type']), self.dist_to_rail(b, d['type'])
        if db < da:
            return b, a
        return a, b

    # ---------- structural predicates ----------
    def loaded(self, m, x):
        """node x has a load relative to device m (pull-up for NMOS, pull-down for PMOS)"""
        for e, t in self.by_net[x]:
            if e is m:
                continue
            if e['kind'] == 'mos':
                if t not in 'ds':
                    continue
                o = e['terms']['s' if t == 'd' else 'd']
                if e['terms']['g'] == x:   # diode
                    return True
                if m['type'] == 'n':
                    if e['type'] == 'p':
                        return True
                    if o == self.supply:
                        return True  # active inductor / follower load
                else:
                    if e['type'] == 'n':
                        return True
            elif e['kind'] == 'res':
                o = e['terms']['n' if t == 'p' else 'p']
                if (m['type'] == 'n' and o == self.supply) or (m['type'] == 'p' and o == self.ground):
                    return True
        return False

    def pull_gates(self, x, mtype):
        rail = self.supply if mtype == 'p' else self.ground
        gates = set(); seen = {x}; q = [x]
        while q:
            y = q.pop()
            if y == rail:
                continue
            for d, t in self.by_net[y]:
                if d['kind'] == 'mos' and d['type'] == mtype and t in 'ds':
                    o = d['terms']['s' if t == 'd' else 'd']
                    gates.add(d['terms']['g'])
                    if o not in seen:
                        seen.add(o); q.append(o)
        return gates

    def static_cmos(self, x):
        pu = self.pull_gates(x, 'p'); pd = self.pull_gates(x, 'n')
        return bool(pu) and pu == pd

    def cm_node(self, y):
        """common-mode node: >=2 resistors from distinct non-rail nets, everything else is R/C to a rail."""
        if self.is_rail(y):
            return False
        rs = set(); other_ok = True
        for e, t in self.by_net[y]:
            if e['kind'] == 'res':
                o = e['terms']['n' if t == 'p' else 'p']
                if self.is_rail(o):
                    continue
                rs.add(o)
            elif e['kind'] == 'cap':
                o = e['terms']['n' if t == 'p' else 'p']
                if not self.is_rail(o):
                    other_ok = False
            else:
                other_ok = False
        return len(rs) >= 2 and other_ok

    def common_source_node(self, x, exclude=None):
        """x is the effective source of >=2 other MOS"""
        cnt = 0
        for e, t in self.by_net[x]:
            if e is exclude or e['kind'] != 'mos' or t not in 'ds':
                continue
            s, dd = self.eff[e['path']]
            if s == x:
                cnt += 1
        return cnt >= 2

    def pair_node(self, x, exclude=None):
        """x is the common source of >=2 MOS whose drains are loaded (a differential pair)"""
        cnt = 0
        for e, t in self.by_net[x]:
            if e is exclude or e['kind'] != 'mos' or t not in 'ds':
                continue
            s, dd = self.eff[e['path']]
            if s == x and not self.is_rail(dd) and self.loaded(e, dd):
                cnt += 1
        return cnt >= 2

    def tg_partner(self, d):
        if d['kind'] != 'mos':
            return None
        a, b = d['terms']['d'], d['terms']['s']
        if self.is_rail(a) or self.is_rail(b):
            return None
        for e, t in self.by_net[a]:
            if e is d or e['kind'] != 'mos' or e['type'] == d['type'] or t not in 'ds':
                continue
            if {e['terms']['d'], e['terms']['s']} == {a, b}:
                return e
        return None

    # ---------- tracing ----------
    def trace(self, starts, cls):
        reached = {}
        events = []
        q = collections.deque()
        for s in starts:
            reached[s] = {'depth': 0, 'via': 'start'}
            q.append(s)
        def add(n, frm, via):
            if n in reached or self.is_rail(n):
                return
            reached[n] = {'depth': reached[frm]['depth'] + 1, 'via': via}
            q.append(n)
        while q:
            x = q.popleft()
            for d, t in self.by_net[x]:
                if d['kind'] == 'mos':
                    s, dr = self.eff[d['path']]
                    g = d['terms']['g']
                    if t == 'g':
                        if g in (d['terms']['d'], d['terms']['s']):
                            events.append((d['path'], 'diode: stop')); continue
                        if self.is_rail(dr):
                            # source follower / active inductor: gate signal -> source
                            if not self.is_rail(s):
                                add(s, x, d['path'] + ' (follower g->s)')
                            continue
                        if not self.loaded(d, dr):
                            events.append((d['path'], 'gate %s: drain %s has no load (tail / steering / stacked switch): stop' % (x, dr)))
                            continue
                        if cls == 'clock' and self.is_rail(s) and not self.static_cmos(dr):
                            events.append((d['path'], 'gate %s: reset/precharge (rail source, dynamic node %s): stop' % (x, dr)))
                            continue
                        add(dr, x, d['path'] + ' (g->d)')
                    elif t in 'ds':
                        p = self.tg_partner(d)
                        if p is not None:
                            o = d['terms']['s' if t == 'd' else 'd']
                            add(o, x, d['path'] + ' (TG channel)')
                            continue
                        if x == s and not self.is_rail(s) and not self.is_rail(dr) and self.loaded(d, dr):
                            add(dr, x, d['path'] + ' (stacked s->d)')
                else:
                    o = d['terms']['n' if t == 'p' else 'p']
                    if self.is_rail(o):
                        continue
                    if self.cm_node(o):
                        events.append((d['path'], 'common-mode node %s: stop' % o)); continue
                    add(o, x, d['path'] + ' (series passive)')
        return reached, events

    def clock_pin_devices(self, nets):
        """devices whose gate is in nets and that act as clock pins of latches/samplers"""
        found = []
        for d in self.devs:
            if d['kind'] != 'mos' or d['terms']['g'] not in nets:
                continue
            s, dr = self.eff[d['path']]
            if self.is_rail(s) and self.pair_node(dr, d):
                found.append((d['path'], 'clocked tail'))
            elif self.is_rail(s) and not self.is_rail(dr) and self.loaded(d, dr) and not self.static_cmos(dr) and self.has_cross(dr):
                found.append((d['path'], 'reset/precharge'))
            elif not self.is_rail(s) and self.pair_node(dr, d):
                # steering: sibling sharing the source, different gate, steering into a different pair
                for e, t in self.by_net[s]:
                    if e is d or e['kind'] != 'mos' or t not in 'ds':
                        continue
                    es, edr = self.eff[e['path']]
                    if es == s and edr != dr and e['terms']['g'] != d['terms']['g'] and self.pair_node(edr, e):
                        found.append((d['path'], 'clock steering')); break
        return found

    def has_cross(self, x, depth=2):
        """node x is (or is one stacked device away from) a cross-coupled node"""
        frontier = {x}
        for _ in range(depth):
            nxt = set()
            for y in frontier:
                if self.is_cross_node(y):
                    return True
                for e, t in self.by_net[y]:
                    if e['kind'] == 'mos' and t in 'ds':
                        nxt.add(e['terms']['s' if t == 'd' else 'd'])
            frontier = nxt - self.rails
        return False

    def is_cross_node(self, y):
        for a, t in self.by_net[y]:
            if a['kind'] != 'mos' or t != 'd':
                continue
            for b, t2 in self.by_net[a['terms']['g']]:
                if b is not a and b['kind'] == 'mos' and t2 == 'd' and b['type'] == a['type'] and b['terms']['g'] == y:
                    return True
        return False



# ======================================================================================
def gate_users(F, n):
    return [d for d, t in F.by_net[n] if d['kind'] == 'mos' and t == 'g']


def classify(F, cells, top, hs_inputs):
    top_ports = cells[top]['ports']
    data_r, data_ev = F.trace(hs_inputs, 'data')
    cand = [p for p in top_ports if p not in F.rails and p not in hs_inputs and p not in data_r]
    clock_inputs, clk_evidence = [], {}
    for p in cand:
        r, ev = F.trace([p], 'clock')
        pins = F.clock_pin_devices(set(r))
        if pins:
            clock_inputs.append(p)
            clk_evidence[p] = pins
    clk_r, clk_ev = F.trace(clock_inputs, 'clock')
    all_nets = set(F.by_net) | set(top_ports)
    netclass = {}
    for n in all_nets:
        cs = []
        if n in F.rails: cs.append('power')
        if n in data_r: cs.append('data')
        if n in clk_r: cs.append('clock')
        netclass[n] = cs
    # switches
    switch_info = {}
    for n in all_nets:
        if netclass[n]:
            continue
        users = gate_users(F, n)
        if not users:
            continue
        sw = []
        for d in users:
            s, dr = F.eff[d['path']]
            if d['terms']['g'] in (d['terms']['d'], d['terms']['s']):
                continue
            if F.is_rail(s) or F.is_rail(dr):
                continue
            sw.append(d)
        if sw and len(sw) == len(users):
            switch_info[n] = sw
    depth = {k: v['depth'] for k, v in data_r.items()}
    depth.update({k: v['depth'] for k, v in clk_r.items() if k not in depth})

    def srcs_at(x):
        return [e for e, t in F.by_net[x] if e['kind'] == 'mos' and t in 'ds' and F.eff[e['path']][0] == x]

    control_info = {}
    for n, sws in switch_info.items():
        kinds = []
        btypes = set()
        for d in sws:
            s, dr = F.eff[d['path']]
            # bundle type: series with current source -> I, series with cap -> C, else R
            series = [e for e, t in F.by_net[s] if e is not d and (e['kind'] != 'mos' or t in 'ds')]
            if any(e['kind'] == 'mos' and F.is_rail(F.eff[e['path']][0]) for e in series) and len(series) == 1:
                btypes.add('I')
            elif any(e['kind'] == 'cap' for e in series) and len(series) == 1:
                btypes.add('C')
            else:
                btypes.add('R')
            prs = [p for p in srcs_at(dr) if p is not d]
            if len(prs) >= 2 and all(p['terms']['g'] in data_r or p['terms']['g'] in clk_r for p in prs):
                gates = sorted({p['terms']['g'] for p in prs})
                drains = {F.eff[p['path']][1] for p in prs}
                others = []
                for x in drains:
                    for e, t in F.by_net[x]:
                        if e['kind'] == 'mos' and t in 'ds' and F.eff[e['path']][1] == x and e not in prs and e['type'] == prs[0]['type']:
                            if e['terms']['g'] in data_r or e['terms']['g'] in clk_r:
                                others.append(e)
                if not others:
                    kinds.append(('general', 'tail of the only pair at %s' % ','.join(sorted(drains))))
                    continue
                allg = {}
                for e in others + prs:
                    allg.setdefault(F.eff[e['path']][0], set()).add(e['terms']['g'])
                main_src = min(allg, key=lambda k: min(depth.get(g, 999) for g in allg[k]))
                main_g = allg[main_src]
                if set(gates) != main_g and F.eff[prs[0]['path']][0] != main_src:
                    kinds.append(('important', 'switch -> current of pair %s, summed at %s with main pair %s' % (
                        '/'.join(gates), '/'.join(sorted(drains)), '/'.join(sorted(main_g)))))
                else:
                    kinds.append(('general', 'switch -> current of an extra pair with the SAME inputs %s as the main pair (gain only)' % '/'.join(gates)))
            else:
                kinds.append(('general', 'switch across %s-%s (degeneration bank, not a summing branch)' % (s, dr)))
        k = 'important' if any(k == 'important' for k, _ in kinds) else 'general'
        control_info[n] = {'kind': k, 'why': sorted({w for _, w in kinds}), 'bundle_type': sorted(btypes),
                           'switches': [d['path'] for d in sws]}
        netclass[n] = ['important_control' if k == 'important' else 'general_control']
    bias_why = {}
    for n in all_nets:
        if netclass[n]:
            continue
        users = gate_users(F, n)
        diode = any(d['kind'] == 'mos' and d['terms']['g'] == n and n in (d['terms']['d'], d['terms']['s']) for d, t in F.by_net[n])
        if F.cm_node(n):
            netclass[n] = ['bias']; bias_why[n] = 'common-mode node of a termination'; continue
        if diode:
            netclass[n] = ['bias']; bias_why[n] = 'diode-connected (mirror) node'; continue
        if users:
            what = sorted({('active-inductor gate' if F.is_rail(F.eff[d['path']][1]) else 'tail/current source' if F.is_rail(F.eff[d['path']][0]) and not F.loaded(d, F.eff[d['path']][1]) or F.pair_node(F.eff[d['path']][1], d) or any(F.eff[e['path']][0] == F.eff[d['path']][1] for e, t in F.by_net[F.eff[d['path']][1]] if e is not d and e['kind'] == 'mos')
                            else 'load') for d in users})
            netclass[n] = ['bias']; bias_why[n] = 'drives only gates of ' + ', '.join(what); continue
        netclass[n] = ['internal']
    return dict(data_r=data_r, data_ev=data_ev, clk_r=clk_r, clk_ev=clk_ev, clock_inputs=clock_inputs,
                clk_evidence=clk_evidence, netclass=netclass, control_info=control_info, bias_why=bias_why, depth=depth)


def device_role(F, d, data_r, clk_r, netclass):
    if d['kind'] != 'mos':
        a, b = d['terms']['p'], d['terms']['n']
        if F.is_rail(a) or F.is_rail(b):
            if d['kind'] == 'cap':
                return {'role': 'cap_to_rail'}
            o = b if F.is_rail(a) else a
            # R from supply to a gate-only node = active-inductor gate resistor
            if all(t == 'g' for e, t in F.by_net[o] if e is not d):
                return {'role': 'gate_r'}
            return {'role': 'r_to_rail'}
        if F.cm_node(a) or F.cm_node(b):
            return {'role': 'termination'}
        return {'role': 'series_' + d['kind']}
    s, dr = F.eff[d['path']]
    g = d['terms']['g']
    sig = lambda n: n in data_r or n in clk_r
    t = d['type']
    own = F.ground if t == 'n' else F.supply
    if F.is_rail(s) and g == own and not F.is_rail(dr):
        return {'role': 'esd' if sig(dr) else 'off_device'}
    if g in (d['terms']['d'], d['terms']['s']):
        return {'role': 'diode'}
    p = F.tg_partner(d)
    if p is not None:
        return {'role': 'tg', 'partner': p['local']}
    for e, tt in F.by_net[g]:
        if e is not d and e['kind'] == 'mos' and tt == 'd' and e['type'] == t and e['terms']['g'] == d['terms']['d'] and e['inst'] == d['inst']:
            return {'role': 'cross', 'partner': e['local']}
    if F.is_rail(dr):
        return {'role': 'active_inductor' if not sig(g) else 'follower'}
    if sig(g):
        if F.loaded(d, dr):
            if F.is_rail(s) and g in clk_r and not F.static_cmos(dr):
                return {'role': 'reset'}
            if F.static_cmos(dr):
                return {'role': 'logic'}
            return {'role': 'input'}
        # stacked device of a static CMOS gate?
        seen = {dr}; q = [dr]
        while q:
            y = q.pop()
            if F.static_cmos(y):
                return {'role': 'logic'}
            for e, tt in F.by_net[y]:
                if e is not d and e['kind'] == 'mos' and e['type'] == t and tt in 'ds':
                    o = e['terms']['s' if tt == 'd' else 'd']
                    if o not in seen and not F.is_rail(o):
                        seen.add(o); q.append(o)
        if F.is_rail(s) and F.pair_node(dr, d):
            return {'role': 'clocked_tail'}
        if F.pair_node(dr, d):
            return {'role': 'clock_steering'}
        return {'role': 'unknown'}
    gc = netclass.get(g, [])
    if 'general_control' in gc or 'important_control' in gc:
        return {'role': 'switch'}
    if F.is_rail(s):
        feeds = [e for e, tt in F.by_net[dr] if e is not d and e['kind'] == 'mos' and tt in 'ds'
                 and F.eff[e['path']][0] == dr and not F.is_rail(F.eff[e['path']][1])]
        if feeds:
            if all('general_control' in netclass.get(e['terms']['g'], []) or 'important_control' in netclass.get(e['terms']['g'], []) for e in feeds):
                return {'role': 'switched_current_source'}
            return {'role': 'tail'}
        return {'role': 'load'}
    return {'role': 'cascode' if 'bias' in gc else 'unknown'}


# ---------------- groups (primitive units inside a cell) ----------------
def cell_groups(cells, cname, croles, block_roles, rails_local):
    c = cells[cname]
    prims = [e for e in c['elems'] if e['kind'] != 'inst']
    dacs = [e for e in c['elems'] if e['kind'] == 'inst' and block_roles.get(e['cell'], {}).get('role') == 'dac']
    items = [('p', e['name']) for e in prims] + [('x', e['name']) for e in dacs]
    parent = {it: it for it in items}
    def f(x):
        while parent[x] != x:
            parent[x] = parent[parent[x]]; x = parent[x]
        return x
    def u(a, b):
        parent[f(a)] = f(b)
    netitems = collections.defaultdict(list)
    gate_of = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for e in prims:
        if e['kind'] == 'mos':
            for t in 'ds':
                n = e['terms'][t]
                if n not in rails_local:
                    netitems[n].append(('p', e['name']))
            gate_of[e['terms']['g']].append(('p', e['name']))
        else:
            for n in e['terms'].values():
                if n not in rails_local:
                    netitems[n].append(('p', e['name']))
    for e in dacs:
        sub = cells[e['cell']]
        # output pin(s) of the DAC: nets that connect to channels inside the parent
        for p, n in e['pins'].items():
            if n in netitems and n not in rails_local:
                netitems[n].append(('x', e['name']))
    for n, its in netitems.items():
        # a net connecting only to channel terminals joins the items; ports that are inputs of other instances do not matter
        for a in its[1:]:
            u(its[0], a)
    # gate-only attachments (e.g. active-inductor gate resistor joins its transistor):
    # an internal (non-port) net used by exactly one passive and one gate
    for n, its in list(netitems.items()):
        if n in c['ports'] or n not in gate_of:
            continue
        gd = gate_of[n]
        if len(its) == 1 and len(gd) == 1 and its[0][0] == 'p':
            e0 = [p for p in prims if p['name'] == its[0][1]][0]
            if e0['kind'] in ('res', 'cap'):
                u(its[0], gd[0])
    groups = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for it in items:
        groups[f(it)].append(it[1])
    return [sorted(v, key=lambda nm: [x['name'] for x in c['elems']].index(nm)) for v in groups.values()]


def group_role(cells, cname, members, croles, cn, F, prefix):
    c = cells[cname]
    el = {e['name']: e for e in c['elems']}
    rl = collections.Counter(croles[m]['role'] for m in members if m in croles)
    if rl.get('termination') or rl.get('esd'):
        parts = []
        if rl.get('termination'): parts.append('termination')
        if rl.get('esd'): parts.append('ESD')
        return {'role': 'termination_esd' if rl.get('esd') else 'termination', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'R/ESD mark on input line',
                'why': ' + '.join(parts) + ': R from HS input to a common-mode node' + (', MOS pad clamps with gate tied to own rail' if rl.get('esd') else '')}
    if rl and set(rl) == {'tg'}:
        return {'role': 'tg', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'switch (TG)', 'why': 'NMOS+PMOS between the same two nets, gates tied to rails'}
    if rl.get('clocked_tail') and rl.get('cross', 0) >= 4 and rl.get('reset'):
        return {'role': 'strongarm', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'triangle + clock',
                'why': 'clocked tail, input pair, cross-coupled NMOS on input drains + cross-coupled PMOS, reset PMOS'}
    if rl.get('cross', 0) >= 2 and rl.get('clock_steering', 0) >= 2:
        return {'role': 'latch', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'box LATCH', 'why': 'input pair + cross-coupled pair, tails steered by complementary clocks'}
    if rl.get('logic') and set(rl) <= {'logic'}:
        n_p = sum(1 for m in members if el[m].get('type') == 'p')
        n_n = sum(1 for m in members if el[m].get('type') == 'n')
        if n_p == 1 and n_n == 1:
            return {'role': 'inverter', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'triangle (CMOS buffer)', 'why': 'complementary PMOS/NMOS with common gate and drain'}
        return {'role': 'static_cmos_gate', 'conf': 'low', 'symbol': 'box (cell name)', 'why': 'static CMOS gate (pull-up gate set = pull-down gate set): NOT in the dictionary'}
    inputs = [m for m in members if m in croles and croles[m]['role'] == 'input']
    if inputs:
        # pair inputs by structural source node
        loc_eff = {m: F.eff[prefix + m] for m in inputs}
        by_src = collections.defaultdict(list)
        for m in inputs:
            by_src[loc_eff[m][0]].append(m)
        pairs = [v for v in by_src.values() if len(v) == 2]
        singles = [v[0] for v in by_src.values() if len(v) == 1]
        degen = False
        if len(singles) == 2 and not pairs:
            sa = loc_eff[singles[0]][0]; sb = loc_eff[singles[1]][0]
            # sources connected by R/C/switch
            for m in members:
                e = el[m]
                ts = set(e['terms'].values()) if e['kind'] != 'inst' else set()
                if e['kind'] in ('res', 'cap') and {prefix_strip(F, prefix, sa), prefix_strip(F, prefix, sb)} <= ts:
                    degen = True
            pairs = [singles]
        drains = [frozenset(F.eff[prefix + m][1] for m in p) for p in pairs]
        gates = [frozenset(el[m]['terms']['g'] for m in p) for p in pairs]
        if len(pairs) == 1:
            if degen:
                return {'role': 'ctle', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'triangle "CTLE"', 'why': 'differential pair with separate sources joined by R/C (source degeneration)'}
            return {'role': 'cml_buffer', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'triangle "BUF"', 'why': 'single differential pair, common tail, loads'}
        if len(set(drains)) == 1:
            if len(set(gates)) == 1:
                return {'role': 'vga', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'triangle "VGA"', 'why': '%d pairs share outputs, all with the same inputs; extra pairs switched in' % len(pairs)}
            return {'role': 'summer', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'Σ', 'why': '%d pairs with different inputs summed at shared outputs' % len(pairs)}
    return {'role': 'unknown', 'conf': 'low', 'symbol': 'box (cell name)', 'why': 'structure not in dictionary'}


def prefix_strip(F, prefix, n):
    return n[len(prefix):] if n.startswith(prefix) else n


def mirror_partners(cells, cname, F, prefix, pairs_seed):
    """structural P/N mirror: seed with input net pairs, extend through devices with a unique candidate
    (same kind/type/size, all known terminal nets mapped). Returns (net map, device map, unmatched)."""
    c = cells[cname]
    prims = [e for e in c['elems'] if e['kind'] != 'inst']
    nm = {}
    for a, b in pairs_seed:
        nm[a] = b; nm[b] = a
    for e in prims:
        if e['kind'] == 'mos':
            nm.setdefault(e['terms']['b'], e['terms']['b'])
    for p in c['ports']:
        pass
    rails = {F.supply, F.ground}
    for ln in cell_all_nets(c):
        if ln in rails:
            nm[ln] = ln
    dm = {}
    def keys(e):
        return [k for k in e['terms'] if not (e['kind'] == 'mos' and k == 'b')]
    def cands(e):
        out = []
        for f2 in prims:
            if f2['name'] in dm or f2['kind'] != e['kind'] or f2.get('type') != e.get('type'):
                continue
            if e['kind'] == 'mos' and e['params'] != f2['params']:
                continue
            if e['kind'] != 'mos' and e.get('value') != f2.get('value'):
                continue
            ok = True; nk = 0
            if e['kind'] == 'mos':
                for k in keys(e):
                    a = e['terms'][k]
                    if a in nm:
                        nk += 1
                        if f2['terms'][k] != nm[a]:
                            ok = False; break
            else:
                ts = list(e['terms'].values()); ts2 = list(f2['terms'].values())
                for a in ts:
                    if a in nm:
                        nk += 1
                        if nm[a] not in ts2:
                            ok = False; break
            if ok and nk:
                out.append(f2)
        return out
    changed = True
    relax = False
    while True:
        changed = False
        for e in prims:
            if e['name'] in dm:
                continue
            cs = cands(e)
            if len(cs) == 1 or (relax and cs):
                f2 = cs[0]
                dm[e['name']] = f2['name']; dm[f2['name']] = e['name']
                if e['kind'] == 'mos':
                    for k in keys(e):
                        a, b = e['terms'][k], f2['terms'][k]
                        nm.setdefault(a, b); nm.setdefault(b, a)
                else:
                    ts = list(e['terms'].values()); ts2 = list(f2['terms'].values())
                    known = [a for a in ts if a in nm]
                    if len(known) == 1:
                        a = known[0]; oa = [x for x in ts if x != a][0]
                        ob = [x for x in ts2 if x != nm[a]]
                        if ob:
                            nm.setdefault(oa, ob[0]); nm.setdefault(ob[0], oa)
                changed = True
                relax = False
        if not changed:
            if relax:
                break
            relax = True
    unmatched = [e['name'] for e in prims if e['name'] not in dm]
    return nm, dm, unmatched


def cell_all_nets(c):
    s = set(c['ports'])
    for e in c['elems']:
        s |= set(e['pins'].values()) if e['kind'] == 'inst' else set(e['terms'].values())
    return s


def F_local_global(F, prefix, n):
    return n


def main():
    nl, top, outdir = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
    hs_inputs = sys.argv[4:]
    cells, order = parse(nl)
    F = Flat(cells, top)
    insts = instance_tree(cells, top)
    top_ports = cells[top]['ports']
    C = classify(F, cells, top, hs_inputs)
    data_r, clk_r, netclass = C['data_r'], C['clk_r'], C['netclass']

    cellnet = collections.defaultdict(lambda: collections.defaultdict(set))
    cell_insts = collections.defaultdict(list)
    l2g = {}
    for prefix, cname, nm in insts:
        cell_insts[cname].append(prefix)
        l2g[prefix] = nm
        for ln, gn in nm.items():
            for c in netclass.get(gn, ['internal']):
                cellnet[cname][ln].add(c)
    for cname in cells:
        for p in cells[cname]['ports']:
            if not cellnet[cname][p]:
                cellnet[cname][p].add('unused')

    roles = {}
    for cname in cells:
        if not cell_insts[cname]:
            continue
        r = {}
        for prefix in cell_insts[cname]:
            for d in F.devs:
                if d['inst'] != prefix:
                    continue
                rr = device_role(F, d, data_r, clk_r, netclass)
                if d['local'] in r and r[d['local']]['role'] != rr['role']:
                    r[d['local']].setdefault('alt', []).append(rr['role'])
                else:
                    r.setdefault(d['local'], rr)
        roles[cname] = r

    # block roles bottom-up
    block = {}
    groups = {}
    def rails_local(cname):
        pre = cell_insts[cname][0]
        return {ln for ln, gn in l2g[pre].items() if gn in F.rails}
    done = set()
    def solve(cname):
        if cname in done:
            return block[cname]
        c = cells[cname]
        for e in c['elems']:
            if e['kind'] == 'inst':
                solve(e['cell'])
        cn = cellnet[cname]
        hs = any(('data' in v or 'clock' in v) for v in cn.values())
        rl = collections.Counter(v['role'] for v in roles.get(cname, {}).values())
        insts_ = [e for e in c['elems'] if e['kind'] == 'inst']
        prims = [e for e in c['elems'] if e['kind'] != 'inst']
        if not hs:
            if rl.get('diode'):
                b = {'role': 'bias', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'omit', 'why': 'diode/mirror devices only; no high-speed net'}
            elif rl.get('switch') and (rl.get('switched_current_source') or rl.get('tail')):
                b = {'role': 'dac', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'variable current source (concept symbol in parent)',
                     'why': 'repeated (bias current source + series switch) units sharing one output'}
            else:
                b = {'role': 'no_hs', 'conf': 'low', 'symbol': 'omit', 'why': 'no high-speed net'}
            block[cname] = b; done.add(cname); return b
        pre = cell_insts[cname][0]
        gs = cell_groups(cells, cname, roles.get(cname, {}), block, rails_local(cname))
        ginfo = []
        for gm in gs:
            gr = group_role(cells, cname, [m for m in gm if m in roles.get(cname, {})], roles.get(cname, {}), cn, F, pre)
            gr['members'] = gm
            ginfo.append(gr)
        groups[cname] = ginfo
        nondac = [e for e in insts_ if block.get(e['cell'], {}).get('role') != 'dac']
        if not nondac and len(ginfo) == 1:
            b = dict(ginfo[0]); b.pop('members', None)
        elif not prims and len(nondac) == 2 and nondac[0]['cell'] == nondac[1]['cell']:
            a, bb = nondac
            sub = block[a['cell']]['role']
            subports = cells[a['cell']]['ports']
            # series: an output pin net of a is an input pin net of b
            link = set(a['pins'].values()) & set(bb['pins'].values())
            if sub == 'latch':
                ca = (a['pins'].get('CLK'), a['pins'].get('CLKB'))
                cb = (bb['pins'].get('CLK'), bb['pins'].get('CLKB'))
                # identify clock pins structurally: pins that are clock-only in the latch cell
                b = {'role': 'dff', 'conf': 'high' if ('DFF' in cname.upper() or 'FF' in cname.upper()) else 'low',
                     'symbol': 'box DFF', 'why': 'two latches in series (master->slave) with complementary clocks'}
            elif sub == 'static_cmos_gate':
                b = {'role': 'cross_coupled_gates', 'conf': 'low', 'symbol': 'box (cell name)',
                     'why': 'two static CMOS gates cross-coupled (SR latch); NOT in the dictionary'}
            else:
                b = {'role': 'composite', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'box (cell name)', 'why': 'two sub-blocks'}
        else:
            b = {'role': 'composite', 'conf': 'high', 'symbol': 'box (cell name)',
                 'why': 'hierarchical: %d sub-blocks + %d primitive unit(s); not itself a dictionary block' % (len(nondac), len(ginfo))}
        block[cname] = b; done.add(cname)
        return b
    solve(top)
    for cname in cells:
        if cell_insts[cname] and cname not in done:
            solve(cname)

    # latch pairs inside composite cells: divider check (feedback loop)
    findings = []
    for cname in cells:
        c = cells[cname]
        latches = [e for e in c['elems'] if e['kind'] == 'inst' and block.get(e['cell'], {}).get('role') == 'latch']
        if len(latches) >= 2 and block.get(cname, {}).get('role') != 'dff':
            outs = {}
            ins = {}
            for e in latches:
                lc = cellnet[e['cell']]
                # data inputs/outputs of the latch cell by trace direction (first instance)
                outs[e['name']] = [e['pins'][p] for p in ('QP', 'QN') if p in e['pins']]
                ins[e['name']] = [e['pins'][p] for p in ('DP', 'DN') if p in e['pins']]
            for a in latches:
                for b2 in latches:
                    if a is b2: continue
                    if set(outs[a['name']]) & set(ins[b2['name']]):
                        loop = bool(set(outs[b2['name']]) & set(ins[a['name']]))
                        clkpins_a = {a['pins'].get('CLK'), a['pins'].get('CLKB')}
                        d_is_clock = bool(set(ins[a['name']]) & clkpins_a)
                        findings.append({'cell': cname, 'master': a['name'], 'slave': b2['name'], 'feedback_loop': loop,
                                         'master_D_is_its_own_clock': d_is_clock,
                                         'name_hint_divider': any('DIV' in x['name'].upper() for x in (a, b2))})

    # mirror (symmetry) analysis for differential unit cells
    sym = {}
    # seeds are derived structurally: input-pair gate nets of each cell (no name rule)
    sym = {}
    for cname in cells:
        if not cell_insts[cname] or block.get(cname, {}).get('role') in ('bias', 'dac'):
            continue
        pre = cell_insts[cname][0]
        r = roles.get(cname, {})
        el = {e['name']: e for e in cells[cname]['elems']}
        ins = [m for m, v in r.items() if v['role'] in ('input',) and el[m]['kind'] == 'mos']
        seed = []
        bysrc = collections.defaultdict(list)
        for m in ins:
            bysrc[F.eff[pre + m][0]].append(m)
        for v in bysrc.values():
            if len(v) == 2 and el[v[0]]['type'] == el[v[1]]['type']:
                seed.append((el[v[0]]['terms']['g'], el[v[1]]['terms']['g']))
        if not seed:
            continue
        nm, dm, un = mirror_partners(cells, cname, F, pre, seed)
        sym[cname] = {'seed': seed, 'unmatched_devices': un}

    res = {
        'top': top, 'rails': {'supply': F.supply, 'ground': F.ground},
        'hs_inputs': hs_inputs, 'clock_inputs': C['clock_inputs'], 'clock_evidence': C['clk_evidence'],
        'netclass': {k: v for k, v in sorted(netclass.items())},
        'bias_why': C['bias_why'],
        'data_trace': {k: v for k, v in sorted(data_r.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]['depth'])},
        'clock_trace': {k: v for k, v in sorted(clk_r.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]['depth'])},
        'data_events': C['data_ev'], 'clock_events': C['clk_ev'],
        'control_info': C['control_info'],
        'cellnet': {c: {n: sorted(v) for n, v in d.items()} for c, d in cellnet.items()},
        'cell_insts': cell_insts,
        'roles': roles, 'block': block, 'groups': groups, 'latch_pairs': findings, 'symmetry': sym,
        'eff': F.eff, 'l2g': l2g,
    }
    json.dump(res, open(outdir + '/analysis.json', 'w'), indent=1, default=list)
    print('clock inputs', C['clock_inputs'])
    for c in cells:
        print(c, block.get(c))
        for g in groups.get(c, []):
            print('    group', g['role'], g['members'])
    print('latch pairs', findings)
    print('symmetry', sym)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
````

````python
"""Plan section 8 step 2: classification confirmation table + questions -> annotations.json, classification.md"""
import json, sys, re, collections
from netlist import parse

W = sys.argv[1]
OUT = sys.argv[2]
NL = sys.argv[3]
A = json.load(open(W + '/analysis.json'))
cells, order = parse(NL)
top = A['top']
nc = A['netclass']
tp = cells[top]['ports']
topnets = sorted({n for n in nc if '/' not in n})
internal_top = [n for n in topnets if n not in tp]


def cls(n):
    return nc.get(n, ['?'])


hs_in = A['hs_inputs']
clk_in = A['clock_inputs']
hs_out = [p for p in tp if ('data' in cls(p) or 'clock' in cls(p)) and p not in hs_in and p not in clk_in]
imp = [p for p in tp if 'important_control' in cls(p)]
gen = [p for p in tp if 'general_control' in cls(p)]
bias_ports = [p for p in tp if 'bias' in cls(p)]
bias_int = [n for n in internal_top if 'bias' in cls(n)]
power = [p for p in tp if 'power' in cls(p)]


def bus_name(names):
    # group names like W1_0..W4_3 into W<4:1>_<3:0> (structure: same switch type, same function)
    pat = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for n in names:
        m = re.match(r'^(\D*?)(\d+)_(\d+)$', n)
        if m:
            pat[m.group(1)].append((int(m.group(2)), int(m.group(3))))
    out = []
    for k, v in pat.items():
        a = sorted({x for x, _ in v}); b = sorted({y for _, y in v})
        out.append('%s<%d:%d>_<%d:%d>' % (k, a[-1], a[0], b[-1], b[0]))
    return out


imp_bus = bus_name(imp)
blocks = A['block']
omitted_blocks = []
for prefix_list in [A['cell_insts']]:
    pass
# omitted blocks at top level: instances whose cell has no HS net
for e in cells[top]['elems']:
    if e['kind'] == 'inst' and blocks[e['cell']]['symbol'] == 'omit':
        omitted_blocks.append('%s (%s: %s)' % (e['name'], e['cell'], blocks[e['cell']]['role']))

# ---------------- questions (low confidence only) ----------------
Q = []
# control name vs structure
for n, ci in sorted(A['control_info'].items()):
    pass
cmis = [n for n in gen if A['control_info'][n]['bundle_type'] == ['R'] and n[0].upper() == 'C']
if cmis:
    Q.append({'id': 'Q1', 'item': ', '.join(cmis) + ' (CTLE_STAGE)',
              'question': '이름(C*)은 커패시터 뱅크 제어처럼 보이지만, 구조상 스위치 MC*P/MC*N은 SP-C*_MID-SN 직렬 경로(저항 경로)이고 C*_CAP은 SP-SN에 항상 연결된 고정 C입니다. 구조대로 그릴까요? (예: 가변 R + 고정 C / 아니오: 가변 C로)',
              'assumed': '예 — 구조를 따름. C0..C2 스위치는 가변 R 묶음에 포함, C_DEG_BASE+C0_CAP+C1_CAP+C2_CAP은 고정 C 하나(합계 330f)로 그림. C0..C2는 일반 제어(그리지 않음).',
              'confidence': 'low (이름-구조 불일치)'})
for f in A['latch_pairs']:
    if f['name_hint_divider'] and not f['feedback_loop']:
        Q.append({'id': 'Q2', 'item': '%s / %s in %s' % (f['master'], f['slave'], f['cell']),
                  'question': '인스턴스 이름은 ÷2 분주기(DIV)이지만 구조상 귀환 루프가 없습니다 (master의 D 입력 = 자기 클럭 B1N/B1P, slave 출력은 어디로도 되돌아가지 않음). 구조대로 "LATCH 2개 직렬"로 그릴까요? (예 / ÷2로 그림 / netlist 오류)',
                  'assumed': '예 — 구조대로 LATCH 박스 2개와 실제 연결을 그림. ÷2 기호는 쓰지 않음. netlist 오류 가능성을 보고서에 남김.',
                  'confidence': 'low (이름-구조 불일치, 사전의 ÷2 = latch 루프와 불일치)'})
tgs = [g for g in A['groups'].get(top, []) if g['role'] == 'tg']
if tgs:
    Q.append({'id': 'Q3', 'item': ', '.join('/'.join(g['members']) for g in tgs),
              'question': '전달 게이트(NMOS 게이트=VSS, PMOS 게이트=VDD)라 항상 꺼져 있습니다. 그러면 O_DOUTP/N(= X_DFE_EVEN의 탭1 귀환 입력 T1_FB)은 떠 있습니다. 의도된 것입니까? (의도됨 / 연결(항상 ON)이어야 함)',
              'assumed': '끊긴 것으로 처리(계획서 §3.2): TG는 열린 스위치 기호 + "(off)". O_DOUTP/N은 구동이 없지만 X_DFE_EVEN의 고속 핀(T1_FB)에 붙어 있어 고속 데이터로 그리고 이름표로 끝냄.',
              'confidence': 'low (구조상 항상 OFF)'})
for c, s in A['symmetry'].items():
    if s['unmatched_devices']:
        Q.append({'id': 'Q4', 'item': '%s: %s' % (c, ', '.join(s['unmatched_devices'])),
                  'question': '%s에 거울 짝이 없는 소자가 있습니다 (M11: PMOS, S=VDD, D=DP, G=DN; 반대쪽 D=DN, G=DP 소자 없음). 차동 회로가 비대칭이 됩니다. 의도된 것입니까? (의도됨 / netlist 오류)' % c,
                  'assumed': '그대로 그림 (정확성 > 대칭, 계획서 §5.2-7). M11은 연결된 노드(DP) 쪽 바깥에 두고, 게이트(DN)는 이름표로 연결, "비대칭" 표시.' % (),
                  'confidence': 'low (대칭 위반이 netlist에 있음)'})
unknown = [c for c, b in blocks.items() if b['conf'] == 'low' and A['cell_insts'].get(c)]
if unknown:
    Q.append({'id': 'Q5', 'item': ', '.join('%s (%s)' % (c, blocks[c]['role']) for c in unknown),
              'question': '블록 역할 사전에 없는 구조입니다 (정적 CMOS 게이트, 교차 결합 게이트 = SR latch). 박스(셀 이름)로 그릴까요? (예 / 역할 지정)',
              'assumed': '예 — 사전 규칙대로 박스(셀 이름) + 보이는 핀. 유닛 레벨은 소자 그대로.',
              'confidence': 'low (사전에 없음)'})
dang = sorted({n.split('/')[-1] for n in A.get('dangling_hs_nets', [])})
# dangling at the top level: HS nets with a single connection
topconn = collections.Counter()
for e in cells[top]['elems']:
    vals = e['pins'].values() if e['kind'] == 'inst' else e['terms'].values()
    for v in vals:
        topconn[v] += 1
dtop = [n for n in internal_top if ('data' in cls(n) or 'clock' in cls(n)) and topconn[n] == 1]
Q.append({'id': 'Q6', 'item': 'top: %s; CLK_DIST_TREE: CLKI_BUF, CLKIB_BUF, CLKQ_BUF, CLKQB_BUF; STRONGARM_SLICER: QPB, QNB' % ', '.join(dtop),
          'question': '추적상 고속 넷이지만 아무 곳에도 연결되지 않은(부하 없는) 출력입니다. 그릴까요? (그림 / 생략)',
          'assumed': '그림 — 분류가 고속이므로 §6.3(생략은 분류대로만)에 따라 생략하지 않고, 끝에 넷 이름표만 둔 짧은 선으로 표시.',
          'confidence': 'low (계획서에 규칙 없음)'})
Q.append({'id': 'Q7', 'item': ', '.join(imp_bus),
          'question': '중요 제어 16비트(W1_0..W4_3)를 "DFE 탭 계수" 기능 하나의 버스로 묶을까요, 탭별 4비트 버스 4개로 나눌까요? (16b 1개 / 4b×4)',
          'assumed': '16b 버스 1개 (W<4:1>_<3:0>) — §4.4 "기능별 버스 하나"를 "DFE 탭 계수" 기능으로 해석.',
          'confidence': 'low (계획서 해석)'})

ann = {
    'hs_inputs': hs_in, 'clock_inputs': clk_in, 'hs_outputs': hs_out,
    'important_controls': imp, 'show_bias': [], 'hide': [],
    'block_roles': {c: b['role'] for c, b in blocks.items() if A['cell_insts'].get(c)},
    '_sources': {'hs_inputs': 'user answer (given)', 'others': 'automatic structural analysis + assumed answers below'},
    '_important_control_buses': {imp_bus[0]: {'bits': imp, 'function': 'DFE tap 1-4 coefficients (IDAC_4BIT switches -> tap-pair tail current)'}} if imp_bus else {},
    '_general_controls': gen, '_bias': bias_ports + bias_int, '_power': power,
    '_questions_and_assumed_answers': Q,
}
json.dump(ann, open(OUT + '/annotations.json', 'w'), indent=1, ensure_ascii=False)

# --------------- markdown ---------------
L = []
L.append('# 분류 확인 표 — %s\n' % top)
L.append('```')
L.append('고속 데이터 입력 : %-40s (확신 높음 — 사용자 지정, 구조 일치: CTLE 입력쌍 게이트 + 종단/ESD 패드)' % ', '.join(hs_in))
L.append('클럭 입력        : %-40s (확신 높음 — 구조: latch 클럭 스티어링·StrongARM 꼬리/리셋 게이트까지 도달, 이름 일치)' % ', '.join(clk_in))
L.append('고속 출력        : %s' % ', '.join(hs_out))
L.append('중요 제어 (버스) : %s (16b) — DFE 탭1~4 계수 (IDAC 스위치 → 탭 쌍 꼬리 전류, 탭 입력 = 귀환 데이터, summer 출력에서 합산)  (확신 높음; 묶음 방식은 Q7)' % ', '.join(imp_bus))
L.append('생략             : 제어 %d개 (%s), 바이어스 %d개 (%s), 전원 %d개 (%s), 블록 %d개 (%s)' % (
    len(gen), ', '.join(gen), len(bias_ports + bias_int), ', '.join(bias_ports + bias_int), len(power), ', '.join(power),
    len(omitted_blocks), ', '.join(omitted_blocks)))
L.append('질문             : %s' % ' / '.join('%s %s' % (q['id'], q['item']) for q in Q))
L.append('```\n')
L.append('## 질문과 가정한 답 (실행 중 답할 사람이 없어 가장 그럴듯한 답을 선택하여 annotations.json에 저장)\n')
for q in Q:
    L.append('- **%s** `%s` — %s\n  - 확신: %s\n  - 가정한 답: %s' % (q['id'], q['item'], q['question'], q['confidence'], q['assumed']))
L.append('\n## 최상위 포트 분류\n')
L.append('| 포트 | 분류 | 근거 |')
L.append('|---|---|---|')
dt = A['data_trace']; ct = A['clock_trace']
def reason(n):
    c = cls(n)
    if n in hs_in:
        return '사용자 지정 고속 입력 (구조 확인: CTLE1 입력쌍 게이트, 종단 R→공통모드, ESD)'
    if n in clk_in:
        return '클럭 입력: 구조 추적이 latch 클럭 스티어링/StrongARM 꼬리·리셋 게이트에 도달 (+ 이름 힌트 CLK)'
    if 'data' in c:
        return '고속 데이터: %s 에서 추적 (via %s)' % ('RX_INP/RX_INN', dt[n]['via'])
    if 'clock' in c:
        return '고속 클럭: CLK_INP/N 에서 추적 (via %s)' % ct[n]['via']
    if 'important_control' in c:
        return '중요 제어: ' + '; '.join(A['control_info'][n]['why'])
    if 'general_control' in c:
        return '일반 제어: ' + '; '.join(A['control_info'][n]['why'][:2]) + (' (이름-구조 불일치 → Q1)' if n in cmis else '')
    if 'bias' in c:
        return '바이어스: ' + A['bias_why'].get(n, '')
    if 'power' in c:
        return '전원 (구조: PMOS/NMOS 벌크 최다 넷)'
    return '?'
for p in tp:
    L.append('| %s | %s | %s |' % (p, '/'.join(cls(p)), reason(p)))
L.append('\n## 최상위 내부 넷 분류\n')
L.append('| 넷 | 분류 | 근거 |')
L.append('|---|---|---|')
for n in internal_top:
    L.append('| %s | %s | %s |' % (n, '/'.join(cls(n)), reason(n)))
L.append('\n## 블록 역할 (셀별)\n')
L.append('| 셀 | 역할 | 기호 | 확신 | 근거 |')
L.append('|---|---|---|---|---|')
for c in order:
    if not A['cell_insts'].get(c):
        continue
    b = blocks[c]
    L.append('| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |' % (c, b['role'], b['symbol'], b['conf'], b.get('why', '')))
L.append('\n## 셀 안의 기본 소자 묶음 (유닛)\n')
for c, gs in A['groups'].items():
    for g in gs:
        L.append('- %s: **%s** — %s — %s' % (c, g['role'], ', '.join(g['members']), g.get('why', '')))
L.append('\n## 참고: 데이터·클럭 겸용 셀\n')
for c, d in A['cellnet'].items():
    mixed = [n for n, v in d.items() if 'data' in v and 'clock' in v]
    if mixed:
        L.append('- %s: 핀 %s 는 DFE 경로에서는 데이터, CLK_DIST_TREE에서는 클럭 (선 굵기는 데이터 기준, 패널에 주석)' % (c, ', '.join(mixed)))
L.append('\n## 추적 중 멈춘 곳 (요약)\n')
ev = collections.Counter()
for d, why in A['data_events'] + A['clock_events']:
    k = re.sub(r'X_[A-Z0-9_/]+/', '', why)
    ev[(d.split('/')[-1], re.sub(r'(gate|drain|node) \S+', r'\1 *', why))] += 1
for (d, w), k in sorted(ev.items()):
    L.append('- %s: %s (×%d)' % (d, w, k))
open(OUT + '/classification.md', 'w').write('\n'.join(L) + '\n')
print('\n'.join(L[:12]))
````

### FILE: reference/rx_afe/work/scene.py
<!-- sha256=6d495d8266147526abd4ecc88d7684837273f7880bed05631adb22ee7effb374 newline=1 -->
````python
"""Scene model: every view = drawing primitives + semantic objects (symbols with pins, wires, dots, labels, ports).
Renderers (HTML/PPTX) only use 'draw' primitives + hotspots; the checker only uses semantic objects + geometry."""
import math

BLACK = '#111'
GRAY = '#777'
CLK_W = 1.6
DATA_W = 1.6
BUS_W = 1.6
SYM_W = 1.6


def fmt_si(v):
    return v


class View:
    def __init__(self, vid, cell, level, title, w, h, scope=None):
        self.id = vid; self.cell = cell; self.level = level; self.title = title
        self.w = w; self.h = h
        self.draw = []          # primitives
        self.symbols = []       # semantic symbols
        self.wires = []
        self.dots = []
        self.labels = []        # net labels / gray labels / ports (all name a net)
        self.texts = []         # every text primitive id (for overlap check) is in draw with 'tid'
        self.hot = []           # clickable hotspots
        self.symmetry = []
        self.allowed_cross = []
        self.notes = []
        self.omitted = []       # list of dicts
        self.scope = scope      # list of member element names covered by this view (None = whole cell)
        self.extra_checks = {}
        self._tid = 0

    # ---------------- primitives ----------------
    def line(self, pts, w=SYM_W, c=BLACK, dash=None, owner=None):
        self.draw.append({'t': 'line', 'pts': [list(map(float, p)) for p in pts], 'w': w, 'c': c, 'dash': dash, 'owner': owner})

    def poly(self, pts, fill=BLACK, stroke=None, w=1.0, owner=None):
        self.draw.append({'t': 'poly', 'pts': [list(map(float, p)) for p in pts], 'fill': fill, 'stroke': stroke, 'w': w, 'owner': owner})

    def circle(self, cx, cy, r, fill='none', stroke=BLACK, w=SYM_W, owner=None):
        self.draw.append({'t': 'circle', 'cx': cx, 'cy': cy, 'r': r, 'fill': fill, 'stroke': stroke, 'w': w, 'owner': owner})

    def rect(self, x, y, w, h, fill='none', stroke=BLACK, sw=SYM_W, dash=None, owner=None, rx=0):
        self.draw.append({'t': 'rect', 'x': x, 'y': y, 'w': w, 'h': h, 'fill': fill, 'stroke': stroke, 'sw': sw, 'dash': dash, 'owner': owner, 'rx': rx})

    def text(self, x, y, s, size=12, anchor='start', color=BLACK, weight='normal', italic=False, cls='lbl', owner=None, base='middle'):
        self._tid += 1
        tid = '%s_t%d' % (self.id, self._tid)
        self.draw.append({'t': 'text', 'x': x, 'y': y, 's': s, 'size': size, 'anchor': anchor, 'color': color,
                          'weight': weight, 'italic': italic, 'cls': cls, 'owner': owner, 'tid': tid, 'base': base})
        return tid

    # ---------------- semantic ----------------
    def wire(self, pts, cls='data', net=None):
        pts = [list(map(float, p)) for p in pts]
        w = {'data': DATA_W, 'clock': CLK_W, 'bus': BUS_W, 'plain': DATA_W, 'power': DATA_W}[cls]
        self.wires.append({'pts': pts, 'cls': cls, 'net': net})
        self.draw.append({'t': 'line', 'pts': pts, 'w': w, 'c': BLACK, 'dash': None, 'owner': 'wire', 'cls': cls})
        return pts

    def dot(self, x, y):
        self.dots.append([float(x), float(y)])
        self.draw.append({'t': 'circle', 'cx': x, 'cy': y, 'r': 3.2, 'fill': BLACK, 'stroke': BLACK, 'w': 0.5, 'owner': 'dot'})

    def netlabel(self, name, ax, ay, tx=None, ty=None, anchor='start', gray=False, size=11, kind='net'):
        """net label: names the wire (or pin) at (ax, ay)"""
        if tx is None:
            tx, ty = ax + 4, ay - 8
        tid = self.text(tx, ty, name, size=size, anchor=anchor, color=GRAY if gray else BLACK, cls='gray' if gray else 'netlabel')
        self.labels.append({'name': name, 'at': [float(ax), float(ay)], 'kind': kind, 'tid': tid})

    def port(self, name, x, y, d='right', io='in', cls='data', label_side=None):
        """Virtuoso pin glyph. (x,y) = attach point (wire end). d = direction the glyph points."""
        L, H = 18, 10
        w = 1.4
        if d == 'right':
            pts = [(x - L, y - H / 2), (x - 6, y - H / 2), (x, y), (x - 6, y + H / 2), (x - L, y + H / 2)]
            if io == 'out':
                pts = [(x, y - H / 2), (x + L - 6, y - H / 2), (x + L, y), (x + L - 6, y + H / 2), (x, y + H / 2)]
        elif d == 'left':
            pts = [(x + L, y - H / 2), (x + 6, y - H / 2), (x, y), (x + 6, y + H / 2), (x + L, y + H / 2)]
            if io == 'out':
                pts = [(x, y - H / 2), (x - L + 6, y - H / 2), (x - L, y), (x - L + 6, y + H / 2), (x, y + H / 2)]
        elif d == 'up':
            pts = [(x - H / 2, y + L), (x - H / 2, y + 6), (x, y), (x + H / 2, y + 6), (x + H / 2, y + L)]
            if io == 'out':
                pts = [(x - H / 2, y), (x - H / 2, y - L + 6), (x, y - L), (x + H / 2, y - L + 6), (x + H / 2, y)]
        else:  # down
            pts = [(x - H / 2, y - L), (x - H / 2, y - 6), (x, y), (x + H / 2, y - 6), (x + H / 2, y - L)]
            if io == 'out':
                pts = [(x - H / 2, y), (x - H / 2, y + L - 6), (x, y + L), (x + H / 2, y + L - 6), (x + H / 2, y)]
        self.poly(pts, fill='#fff', stroke=BLACK, w=w, owner='port')
        xs = [p[0] for p in pts]; ys = [p[1] for p in pts]
        bb = [min(xs), min(ys), max(xs), max(ys)]
        if label_side is None:
            label_side = {'right': 'left' if io == 'in' else 'right', 'left': 'right' if io == 'in' else 'left',
                          'up': 'below' if io == 'in' else 'above', 'down': 'above' if io == 'in' else 'below'}[d]
        if label_side == 'left':
            tid = self.text(bb[0] - 4, y, name, size=12, anchor='end', weight='bold', cls='port')
        elif label_side == 'right':
            tid = self.text(bb[2] + 4, y, name, size=12, anchor='start', weight='bold', cls='port')
        elif label_side == 'above':
            tid = self.text(x, bb[1] - 8, name, size=12, anchor='middle', weight='bold', cls='port')
        else:
            tid = self.text(x, bb[3] + 9, name, size=12, anchor='middle', weight='bold', cls='port')
        self.labels.append({'name': name, 'at': [float(x), float(y)], 'kind': 'port', 'tid': tid, 'io': io})
        self.symbols.append({'id': 'pin_' + name + '_%d' % len(self.symbols), 'kind': 'portglyph', 'body': [bb], 'pins': [], 'members': []})

    # ---------------- symbols ----------------
    def add_symbol(self, sid, kind, body, pins, members, link=None, info=None):
        s = {'id': sid, 'kind': kind, 'body': body, 'pins': pins, 'members': members, 'link': link, 'info': info or {}}
        self.symbols.append(s)
        if link:
            xs = [b[0] for b in body] + [b[2] for b in body]
            ys = [b[1] for b in body] + [b[3] for b in body]
            self.hot.append({'x': min(xs) - 4, 'y': min(ys) - 4, 'w': max(xs) - min(xs) + 8, 'h': max(ys) - min(ys) + 8, 'link': link, 'sym': sid})
        return s


def pin(name, x, y, terms):
    """terms: list (per slice) of list of (element, terminal). For block instances element=inst name, terminal=port."""
    return {'name': name, 'x': float(x), 'y': float(y), 'terms': terms}


# ============================ device symbols ============================
def mos(V, sid, cx, cy, mtype, gate='left', name='', size='', top=None, bot=None, gterm=None, link=None,
        name_side=None, members=None, extra_info=None, arrow_at=None):
    """Razavi 3-terminal MOS. (cx,cy): drain/source column x, center y.
    top/bot/gterm: per-slice lists of (element, terminal) for the top pin, bottom pin and gate.
    Pins: top (cx, cy-30), bottom (cx, cy+30), gate (cx-40 or cx+40, cy)."""
    s = -1 if gate == 'left' else 1
    chx = cx + s * 14
    gpx = cx + s * 20
    V.line([(chx, cy - 18), (chx, cy + 18)], w=2.6, owner=sid)
    V.line([(gpx, cy - 14), (gpx, cy + 14)], w=2.0, owner=sid)
    V.line([(cx + s * 40, cy), (gpx, cy)], w=SYM_W, owner=sid)
    V.line([(chx, cy - 12), (cx, cy - 12), (cx, cy - 30)], w=SYM_W, owner=sid)
    V.line([(chx, cy + 12), (cx, cy + 12), (cx, cy + 30)], w=SYM_W, owner=sid)
    # arrow on the (structural) source lead
    if mtype == 'n':
        ay = cy + 12 if arrow_at != 'top' else cy - 12
        tip = cx - s * 1
        V.poly([(tip, ay), (tip + s * 7, ay - 4), (tip + s * 7, ay + 4)], owner=sid)
    else:
        ay = cy - 12 if arrow_at != 'bottom' else cy + 12
        tip = chx - s * 1
        V.poly([(tip, ay), (tip - s * 7, ay - 4), (tip - s * 7, ay + 4)], owner=sid)
    ns = name_side or ('right' if gate == 'left' else 'left')
    if ns == 'right':
        tx, anc = cx + 6, 'start'
    else:
        tx, anc = cx - 6, 'end'
    if name:
        V.text(tx, cy - 7, name, size=11, anchor=anc, italic=True, cls='devname', owner=sid)
    if size:
        lines = size if isinstance(size, list) else [size]
        for i, ln in enumerate(lines):
            V.text(tx, cy + 7 + i * 12, ln, size=10, anchor=anc, color='#555', cls='size', owner=sid)
    bx0, bx1 = sorted([gpx, chx])
    body = [[bx0 - 1, cy - 18, bx1 + 1, cy + 18]]
    pins = [pin('T', cx, cy - 30, top), pin('B', cx, cy + 30, bot), pin('G', cx + s * 40, cy, gterm)]
    return V.add_symbol(sid, 'nmos' if mtype == 'n' else 'pmos', body, pins, members or [], link,
                        dict(cx=cx, cy=cy, gate=gate, **(extra_info or {})))


def res(V, sid, x, y, orient='v', name='', size='', a=None, b=None, var=False, members=None, label_side='right'):
    if orient == 'v':
        pts = [(x, y - 30), (x, y - 20)]
        n = 6
        for i in range(n):
            yy = y - 20 + (i + 0.5) * 40 / n
            pts.append((x + (6 if i % 2 == 0 else -6), yy))
        pts += [(x, y + 20), (x, y + 30)]
        V.line(pts, owner=sid)
        body = [[x - 7, y - 20, x + 7, y + 20]]
        pins = [pin('A', x, y - 30, a), pin('B', x, y + 30, b)]
    else:
        pts = [(x - 30, y), (x - 20, y)]
        n = 6
        for i in range(n):
            xx = x - 20 + (i + 0.5) * 40 / n
            pts.append((xx, y + (-6 if i % 2 == 0 else 6)))
        pts += [(x + 20, y), (x + 30, y)]
        V.line(pts, owner=sid)
        body = [[x - 20, y - 7, x + 20, y + 7]]
        pins = [pin('A', x - 30, y, a), pin('B', x + 30, y, b)]
    if var:
        _var_arrow(V, x, y, sid)
        body = [[x - 16, y - 16, x + 16, y + 16]]
    _dev_text(V, x, y, orient, name, size, label_side, sid, off=20 if var else 10)
    return V.add_symbol(sid, 'varres' if var else 'res', body, pins, members or [], None, dict(x=x, y=y))


def cap(V, sid, x, y, orient='v', name='', size='', a=None, b=None, var=False, members=None, label_side='right'):
    if orient == 'v':
        V.line([(x, y - 30), (x, y - 4)], owner=sid)
        V.line([(x - 12, y - 4), (x + 12, y - 4)], w=2.4, owner=sid)
        V.line([(x - 12, y + 4), (x + 12, y + 4)], w=2.4, owner=sid)
        V.line([(x, y + 4), (x, y + 30)], owner=sid)
        body = [[x - 12, y - 5, x + 12, y + 5]]
        pins = [pin('A', x, y - 30, a), pin('B', x, y + 30, b)]
    else:
        V.line([(x - 30, y), (x - 4, y)], owner=sid)
        V.line([(x - 4, y - 12), (x - 4, y + 12)], w=2.4, owner=sid)
        V.line([(x + 4, y - 12), (x + 4, y + 12)], w=2.4, owner=sid)
        V.line([(x + 4, y), (x + 30, y)], owner=sid)
        body = [[x - 5, y - 12, x + 5, y + 12]]
        pins = [pin('A', x - 30, y, a), pin('B', x + 30, y, b)]
    if var:
        _var_arrow(V, x, y, sid)
        body = [[x - 16, y - 16, x + 16, y + 16]]
    _dev_text(V, x, y, orient, name, size, label_side, sid, off=16 if var else 14)
    return V.add_symbol(sid, 'varcap' if var else 'cap', body, pins, members or [], None, dict(x=x, y=y))


def _var_arrow(V, x, y, sid):
    V.line([(x - 15, y + 15), (x + 14, y - 14)], w=1.3, owner=sid)
    V.poly([(x + 16, y - 16), (x + 7, y - 13), (x + 13, y - 7)], owner=sid)


def _dev_text(V, x, y, orient, name, size, side, sid, off=10):
    lines = size if isinstance(size, list) else ([size] if size else [])
    if orient == 'v':
        if side == 'right':
            tx, anc = x + off + 2, 'start'
        else:
            tx, anc = x - off - 2, 'end'
        if name:
            V.text(tx, y - 7, name, size=11, anchor=anc, italic=True, cls='devname', owner=sid)
        for i, ln in enumerate(lines):
            V.text(tx, y + 7 + 12 * i, ln, size=10, anchor=anc, color='#555', cls='size', owner=sid)
    else:
        if side == 'above':
            if name:
                V.text(x, y - off - 6 - 12 * len(lines), name, size=11, anchor='middle', italic=True, cls='devname', owner=sid)
            for i, ln in enumerate(lines):
                V.text(x, y - off - 6 - 12 * (len(lines) - 1 - i), ln, size=10, anchor='middle', color='#555', cls='size', owner=sid)
        else:
            if name:
                V.text(x, y + off + 8, name, size=11, anchor='middle', italic=True, cls='devname', owner=sid)
            for i, ln in enumerate(lines):
                V.text(x, y + off + 20 + 12 * i, ln, size=10, anchor='middle', color='#555', cls='size', owner=sid)


def visrc(V, sid, x, y, name='', size='', top=None, bot=None, bus=None, bus_side='right', members=None, label_side='left'):
    """variable current source (circle + arrow + diagonal arrow). pins top (x,y-30), bottom (x,y+30), optional bus pin."""
    V.line([(x, y - 30), (x, y - 16)], owner=sid)
    V.line([(x, y + 16), (x, y + 30)], owner=sid)
    V.circle(x, y, 16, owner=sid)
    V.line([(x, y - 9), (x, y + 6)], w=1.4, owner=sid)
    V.poly([(x, y + 10), (x - 4, y + 3), (x + 4, y + 3)], owner=sid)
    V.line([(x - 20, y + 20), (x + 18, y - 18)], w=1.3, owner=sid)
    V.poly([(x + 21, y - 21), (x + 11, y - 18), (x + 18, y - 11)], owner=sid)
    pins = [pin('T', x, y - 30, top), pin('B', x, y + 30, bot)]
    body = [[x - 21, y - 21, x + 21, y + 21]]
    if bus is not None:
        s = 1 if bus_side == 'right' else -1
        V.line([(x + s * 16, y), (x + s * 40, y)], w=BUS_W, owner=sid)
        pins.append(pin('BUS', x + s * 40, y, bus))
    _dev_text(V, x, y, 'v', name, size, label_side, sid, off=22)
    return V.add_symbol(sid, 'visrc', body, pins, members or [], None, dict(x=x, y=y))


def gnd(V, x, y, net):
    V.line([(x, y), (x, y + 8)], owner='gnd')
    V.line([(x - 10, y + 8), (x + 10, y + 8)], w=1.8, owner='gnd')
    V.line([(x - 6, y + 12), (x + 6, y + 12)], w=1.6, owner='gnd')
    V.line([(x - 2, y + 16), (x + 2, y + 16)], w=1.4, owner='gnd')
    V.labels.append({'name': net, 'at': [float(x), float(y)], 'kind': 'power'})
    V.symbols.append({'id': 'gnd_%d_%d' % (x, y), 'kind': 'gnd', 'body': [[x - 10, y + 7, x + 10, y + 17]], 'pins': [], 'members': []})


def vdd_tee(V, x, y, net, label='VDD'):
    V.line([(x, y), (x, y - 8)], owner='vdd')
    V.line([(x - 10, y - 8), (x + 10, y - 8)], w=1.8, owner='vdd')
    tid = V.text(x, y - 17, label, size=10, anchor='middle', cls='power')
    V.labels.append({'name': net, 'at': [float(x), float(y)], 'kind': 'power', 'tid': tid})
    V.symbols.append({'id': 'vdd_%d_%d' % (x, y), 'kind': 'vdd', 'body': [[x - 10, y - 9, x + 10, y - 7]], 'pins': [], 'members': []})


def rail(V, x0, x1, y, net, label='VDD'):
    V.wire([(x0, y), (x1, y)], cls='power', net=net)
    tid = V.text(x0 - 6, y, label, size=12, anchor='end', weight='bold', cls='power')
    V.labels.append({'name': net, 'at': [float(x0), float(y)], 'kind': 'power', 'tid': tid})


def gray_gate(V, p, name, side):
    """short gray label at a gate pin of an invisible net (no wire)"""
    x, y = p['x'], p['y']
    if side == 'left':
        tid = V.text(x - 3, y, name, size=10, anchor='end', color=GRAY, cls='gray')
    else:
        tid = V.text(x + 3, y, name, size=10, anchor='start', color=GRAY, cls='gray')
    V.labels.append({'name': name, 'at': [x, y], 'kind': 'gray', 'tid': tid})


# ============================ block symbols ============================
def tri(V, sid, x0, yc, label, inst, w=80, h=90, ins=None, outs=None, bottom=None, top=None, link=None,
        clock_mark=False, members=None, sub='', cls='data'):
    """amplifier triangle pointing right. ins/outs: list of (pinname, dy, terms). bottom/top: list of (pinname, dx, terms, cls)"""
    V.poly([(x0, yc - h / 2), (x0 + w, yc), (x0, yc + h / 2)], fill='#fff', stroke=BLACK, w=SYM_W, owner=sid)
    V.text(x0 + w * 0.36, yc, label, size=12, anchor='middle', weight='bold', cls='blk', owner=sid)
    pins = []
    lw = CLK_W if cls == 'clock' else SYM_W
    for (pn, dy, terms) in (ins or []):
        V.line([(x0 - 20, yc + dy), (x0, yc + dy)], w=lw, owner=sid)
        pins.append(pin(pn, x0 - 20, yc + dy, terms))
    for (pn, dy, terms) in (outs or []):
        xe = x0 + w * (1 - abs(dy) / (h / 2))
        V.line([(xe, yc + dy), (x0 + w + 20, yc + dy)], w=lw, owner=sid)
        pins.append(pin(pn, x0 + w + 20, yc + dy, terms))
    for (pn, dx, terms, cl) in (bottom or []):
        ye = yc + (h / 2) * (1 - dx / w)
        V.line([(x0 + dx, ye), (x0 + dx, yc + h / 2 + 20)], w=CLK_W if cl == 'clock' else SYM_W, owner=sid)
        pins.append(pin(pn, x0 + dx, yc + h / 2 + 20, terms))
        if clock_mark:
            V.poly([(x0 + dx - 6, ye), (x0 + dx, ye - 8), (x0 + dx + 6, ye)], fill='none', stroke=BLACK, w=1.2, owner=sid)
    for (pn, dx, terms, cl) in (top or []):
        ye = yc - (h / 2) * (1 - dx / w)
        V.line([(x0 + dx, ye), (x0 + dx, yc - h / 2 - 20)], w=BUS_W if cl == 'bus' else SYM_W, owner=sid)
        pins.append(pin(pn, x0 + dx, yc - h / 2 - 20, terms))
    if inst:
        V.text(x0 + w / 2 - 6, yc - h / 2 - 10, inst, size=10, anchor='middle', italic=True, color='#444', cls='inst', owner=sid)
    if sub:
        if bottom:
            V.text(x0 + w / 2 - 6, yc - h / 2 - 23, sub, size=10, anchor='middle', color='#444', cls='inst', owner=sid)
        else:
            V.text(x0 + w / 2 - 6, yc + h / 2 + 12, sub, size=10, anchor='middle', color='#444', cls='inst', owner=sid)
    body = [[x0, yc - h / 2, x0 + w * 0.5, yc + h / 2], [x0 + w * 0.5, yc - h / 4, x0 + w * 0.85, yc + h / 4]]
    return V.add_symbol(sid, 'tri', body, pins, members or [], link, dict(x0=x0, yc=yc, w=w, h=h))


def box(V, sid, x0, y0, w, h, label, inst, left=None, right=None, bottom=None, top=None, link=None, members=None,
        big=False, pin_text=True, sub=None):
    """block box. left/right: list of (pinname, y, terms, cls); bottom/top: list of (pinname, x, terms, cls). pins at 20px from edge."""
    V.rect(x0, y0, w, h, fill='#fff', stroke=BLACK, sw=SYM_W, owner=sid)
    V.text(x0 + w / 2, y0 + h / 2 - (8 if sub else 0), label, size=22 if big else 13, anchor='middle', weight='bold', cls='blk', owner=sid)
    if sub:
        V.text(x0 + w / 2, y0 + h / 2 + 12, sub, size=10, anchor='middle', color='#444', cls='blk', owner=sid)
    if inst:
        V.text(x0, y0 - 9, inst, size=10, anchor='start', italic=True, color='#444', cls='inst', owner=sid)
    pins = []
    def lw(cl):
        return CLK_W if cl in ('clock', 'bus') else SYM_W
    for (pn, y, terms, cl) in (left or []):
        V.line([(x0 - 20, y), (x0, y)], w=lw(cl), owner=sid)
        pins.append(pin(pn, x0 - 20, y, terms))
        if pin_text:
            V.text(x0 + 4, y, pn, size=9, anchor='start', color='#555', cls='pintext', owner=sid)
    for (pn, y, terms, cl) in (right or []):
        V.line([(x0 + w, y), (x0 + w + 20, y)], w=lw(cl), owner=sid)
        pins.append(pin(pn, x0 + w + 20, y, terms))
        if pin_text:
            V.text(x0 + w - 4, y, pn, size=9, anchor='end', color='#555', cls='pintext', owner=sid)
    for (pn, x, terms, cl) in (bottom or []):
        V.line([(x, y0 + h), (x, y0 + h + 20)], w=lw(cl), owner=sid)
        pins.append(pin(pn, x, y0 + h + 20, terms))
        if pin_text:
            V.text(x, y0 + h - 8, pn, size=9, anchor='middle', color='#555', cls='pintext', owner=sid)
    for (pn, x, terms, cl) in (top or []):
        V.line([(x, y0), (x, y0 - 20)], w=lw(cl), owner=sid)
        pins.append(pin(pn, x, y0 - 20, terms))
        if pin_text:
            V.text(x, y0 + 9, pn, size=9, anchor='middle', color='#555', cls='pintext', owner=sid)
    return V.add_symbol(sid, 'box', [[x0, y0, x0 + w, y0 + h]], pins, members or [], link, dict(x0=x0, y0=y0, w=w, h=h))


def inv(V, sid, x0, yc, inst, a_terms, y_terms, link=None, members=None, flip_label=False, cls='data'):
    """inverter (CMOS buffer) triangle w=36 h=32 + bubble. pins A (x0-20,yc), Y (x0+36+8+20, yc)"""
    w, h = 34, 30
    V.poly([(x0, yc - h / 2), (x0 + w, yc), (x0, yc + h / 2)], fill='#fff', stroke=BLACK, w=SYM_W, owner=sid)
    V.circle(x0 + w + 4, yc, 4, fill='#fff', owner=sid)
    lw = CLK_W if cls == 'clock' else SYM_W
    V.line([(x0 - 20, yc), (x0, yc)], w=lw, owner=sid)
    V.line([(x0 + w + 8, yc), (x0 + w + 28, yc)], w=lw, owner=sid)
    if inst:
        V.text(x0 + 14, yc + (h / 2 + 10) * (-1 if flip_label else 1), inst, size=9, anchor='middle', italic=True, color='#444', cls='inst', owner=sid)
    pins = [pin('A', x0 - 20, yc, a_terms), pin('Y', x0 + w + 28, yc, y_terms)]
    return V.add_symbol(sid, 'inv', [[x0, yc - h / 2, x0 + w * 0.6, yc + h / 2], [x0 + w * 0.6, yc - 6, x0 + w + 8, yc + 6]], pins, members or [], link,
                        dict(x0=x0, yc=yc))


def switch_open(V, sid, x, y, orient, a_terms, b_terms, label, sub, link=None, members=None):
    """open switch glyph (e.g. always-off TG). vertical: pins (x, y-30) top, (x, y+30) bottom"""
    if orient == 'v':
        V.line([(x, y - 30), (x, y - 12)], owner=sid)
        V.line([(x, y + 12), (x, y + 30)], owner=sid)
        V.circle(x, y - 12, 2.5, fill='#fff', owner=sid)
        V.circle(x, y + 12, 2.5, fill='#fff', owner=sid)
        V.line([(x, y + 12), (x + 13, y - 9)], w=1.8, owner=sid)
        pins = [pin('A', x, y - 30, a_terms), pin('B', x, y + 30, b_terms)]
        body = [[x - 4, y - 14, x + 15, y + 14]]
        V.text(x + 20, y - 6, label, size=11, anchor='start', weight='bold', cls='blk', owner=sid)
        V.text(x + 20, y + 8, sub, size=9, anchor='start', color='#555', cls='blk', owner=sid)
    return V.add_symbol(sid, 'switch', body, pins, members or [], link, dict(x=x, y=y))


def term_marker(V, sid, x, y, up, label, terms, esd=False, link=None, members=None):
    """termination (+ESD) marker hanging from a line at (x,y): pin at (x,y). up=True draws above."""
    s = -1 if up else 1
    # stem (a wire from the line to the pin) is drawn by the caller: pin at (x, y + s*12)
    # resistor zigzag
    pts = [(x, y + s * 12)]
    for i in range(5):
        pts.append((x + (5 if i % 2 == 0 else -5), y + s * (14 + i * 5)))
    pts += [(x, y + s * 38), (x, y + s * 44)]
    V.line(pts, owner=sid)
    V.circle(x, y + s * 47, 3, fill='#fff', owner=sid)
    body = [sorted_box(x - 7, y + s * 12, x + 7, y + s * 50)]
    tx = x + 9
    V.text(tx, y + s * 26, label, size=9, anchor='start', color='#333', cls='blk', owner=sid)
    if esd:
        # small ESD diode pair glyph to the right of the resistor
        ex = x + 9; ey = y + s * 40
        V.text(ex, ey, 'ESD', size=9, anchor='start', color='#333', weight='bold', cls='blk', owner=sid)
    return V.add_symbol(sid, 'term', body, [pin('P', x, y + s * 12, terms)], members or [], link, dict(x=x, y=y))


def sorted_box(x0, y0, x1, y1):
    return [min(x0, x1), min(y0, y1), max(x0, x1), max(y0, y1)]
````

### FILE: reference/rx_afe/work/layouts.py
<!-- sha256=70dd702f2bb6e639cc14bc8f47346f6da46818535b79e5eafa94911878a24ae1 newline=1 -->
````python
"""Step 3 (plan sections 4, 5): concept-diagram views. Writes scenes.json
Every template takes element names from the structural analysis (roles), not from the netlist text."""
import json, sys, collections, re
from netlist import parse, num
from scene import *

W = sys.argv[1]
NL = sys.argv[2]
cells, order = parse(NL)
A = json.load(open(W + '/analysis.json'))
ROLES = A['roles']; CN = A['cellnet']; EFF = A['eff']; INST = A['cell_insts']
VIEWS = []


def el(cell, name):
    for e in cells[cell]['elems']:
        if e['name'] == name:
            return e
    raise KeyError(cell + ':' + name)


def role(cell, name):
    return ROLES[cell][name]['role']


def T(dev, t):
    return [[(dev, t)]]


def TM(devs, t):
    return [[(d, t)] for d in devs]


def topbot(cell, dev):
    """terminal letters (top, bottom) for drawing: NMOS top = structural drain, PMOS top = structural source"""
    e = el(cell, dev)
    pre = INST[cell][0]
    s, d = EFF[pre + dev]
    gs = lambda n: n if n in ('VDD', 'VSS') else None
    # map global back to terminal letter
    ts = e['terms']
    g2l = A['l2g'][pre]
    loc = {v: k for k, v in g2l.items()}
    sl = loc.get(s, s)
    st = 's' if ts['s'] == sl else 'd'
    dt = 'd' if st == 's' else 's'
    if e['type'] == 'n':
        return dt, st
    return st, dt


def mosT(cell, devs):
    """top/bottom/gate per-slice terms for one or merged devices"""
    if isinstance(devs, str):
        devs = [devs]
    tb = [topbot(cell, d) for d in devs]
    top = [[(d, t[0])] for d, t in zip(devs, tb)]
    bot = [[(d, t[1])] for d, t in zip(devs, tb)]
    g = [[(d, 'g')] for d in devs]
    return top, bot, g


def wl(cell, devs):
    if isinstance(devs, str):
        devs = [devs]
    es = [el(cell, d) for d in devs]
    Ws = [e['params'].get('w', '?') for e in es]
    Ls = sorted({e['params'].get('l', '?') for e in es}, key=lambda x: num(x) or 0)
    ms = [e['params'].get('m') for e in es]
    if len(es) == 1:
        s = '%s/%s' % (Ws[0], Ls[0])
        if ms[0] and ms[0] != '1':
            s += ' ×%s' % ms[0]
        return s
    return ['W ' + ','.join(Ws), 'L ' + '/'.join(Ls)]


def cls_of(cell, net):
    c = CN[cell].get(net, [])
    if 'data' in c:
        return 'data'
    if 'clock' in c:
        return 'clock'
    if 'important_control' in c:
        return 'bus'
    return 'plain'


def hidden_list(cell, shown=None):
    """omitted (hidden) nets of the cell by class"""
    out = []
    for n, c in sorted(CN[cell].items()):
        k = [x for x in c if x in ('general_control', 'bias', 'power')]
        if k and not any(x in c for x in ('data', 'clock', 'important_control')):
            out.append({'net': n, 'class': '/'.join(k)})
    return out


def gnd_at(V, x, y, net='VSS', l=12):
    V.wire([(x, y), (x, y + l)], cls='power')
    gnd(V, x, y + l, net)


def busslash(V, x, y, n, vertical=True):
    if vertical:
        V.line([(x - 7, y + 7), (x + 7, y - 7)], w=1.4, owner='bus')
        V.text(x + 9, y + 4, str(n), size=10, anchor='start', cls='buswidth')
    else:
        V.line([(x - 7, y + 7), (x + 7, y - 7)], w=1.4, owner='bus')
        V.text(x, y + 16, str(n), size=10, anchor='middle', cls='buswidth')


def legend(V, x, y, lines, title='묶음·병합 (bundles / merged)'):
