<label><input type="checkbox" id="wl" checked> W/L·m 표시</label>
<a class="btn" id="dl" download="concept.pptx">PPT 내려받기</a></div></header>
<main><div id="stage"></div><aside id="side"></aside></main>
<script>
const DATA = __DATA__;
const PPTX = "__PPTX__";
const NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
const byId = {}; DATA.views.forEach(v => byId[v.id] = v);
function el(t, a, p){const e = document.createElementNS(NS, t); for (const k in a) e.setAttribute(k, a[k]); if (p) p.appendChild(e); return e;}
function render(v){
  const svg = el('svg', {viewBox: `0 0 ${v.w} ${v.h}`, width: v.w, height: v.h, 'font-family': '"Liberation Sans",Arial,"Noto Sans KR","WenQuanYi Zen Hei",sans-serif', id: 'sv_' + v.id});
  el('rect', {x: 0, y: 0, width: v.w, height: v.h, fill: '#fff'}, svg);
  for (const d of v.draw){
    if (d.t === 'line'){
      el('polyline', {points: d.pts.map(p => p.join(',')).join(' '), fill: 'none', stroke: d.c, 'stroke-width': d.w,
        'stroke-linejoin': 'miter', 'stroke-linecap': 'butt', 'stroke-dasharray': d.dash || 'none', 'data-owner': d.owner || ''}, svg);
    } else if (d.t === 'poly'){
      el('polygon', {points: d.pts.map(p => p.join(',')).join(' '), fill: d.fill, stroke: d.stroke || 'none', 'stroke-width': d.w}, svg);
    } else if (d.t === 'circle'){
      el('circle', {cx: d.cx, cy: d.cy, r: d.r, fill: d.fill, stroke: d.stroke, 'stroke-width': d.w}, svg);
    } else if (d.t === 'rect'){
      el('rect', {x: d.x, y: d.y, width: d.w, height: d.h, fill: d.fill, stroke: d.stroke, 'stroke-width': d.sw, rx: d.rx || 0}, svg);
    } else if (d.t === 'text'){
      const t = el('text', {x: d.x, y: d.y, 'font-size': d.size, 'text-anchor': d.anchor, fill: d.color, 'font-weight': d.weight,
        'font-style': d.italic ? 'italic' : 'normal', 'dominant-baseline': 'central', class: d.cls, 'data-tid': d.tid}, svg);
      t.textContent = d.s;
    }
  }
  for (const h of v.hot){
    const r = el('rect', {x: h.x, y: h.y, width: h.w, height: h.h, class: 'hot', rx: 4}, svg);
    const tt = el('title', {}, r); tt.textContent = '클릭: ' + (byId[h.link] ? byId[h.link].title : h.link);
    r.addEventListener('click', () => go(h.link));
  }
  return svg;
}
function crumbs(id){const c = []; let x = id; while (x){c.unshift(x); x = DATA.parent[x];} return c;}
function go(id){ if (!byId[id]) id = DATA.views[0].id; if (location.hash !== '#' + id) location.hash = id; else show(id); }
function show(id){
  const v = byId[id] || DATA.views[0];
  document.getElementById('ttl').textContent = v.title;
  const st = document.getElementById('stage'); st.innerHTML = ''; st.appendChild(render(v));
  const cr = document.getElementById('crumb'); cr.innerHTML = '';
  crumbs(v.id).forEach((c, i, arr) => { if (i) {const s = document.createElement('span'); s.className = 'sep'; s.textContent = '›'; cr.appendChild(s);}
    const a = document.createElement('a'); a.textContent = byId[c].cell + (byId[c].level === 'unit' ? ' (unit)' : ''); a.onclick = () => go(c); cr.appendChild(a); });
  document.getElementById('back').disabled = !DATA.parent[v.id];
  const sd = document.getElementById('side'); const S = DATA.summary;
  let h = `<h2>이 화면</h2><div>${v.cell} · ${v.level === 'unit' ? '유닛(MOSFET) 레벨' : '블록 레벨'}</div>`;
  if (v.children.length){ h += '<div class="mut">클릭하면 내려감:</div><ul>' + v.children.map(c => `<li><a onclick="go('${c}')">${byId[c] ? byId[c].title : c}</a></li>`).join('') + '</ul>'; }
  h += '<h2>생략 목록 (이 화면)</h2><ul>';
  if (!v.omitted.length) h += '<li class="mut">없음</li>';
  for (const o of v.omitted){ h += o.net ? `<li>${o.net} — ${o.class}</li>` : `<li>${o.block} (${o.cell}) — ${o.reason}</li>`; }
  h += '</ul>';
  if (v.notes.length) h += '<h2>주석</h2><ul>' + v.notes.map(n => `<li>${n}</li>`).join('') + '</ul>';
  h += `<h2>분류 요약 (전체)</h2><ul><li>고속 데이터 입력: ${S.hs_inputs.join(', ')}</li><li>클럭 입력: ${S.clock_inputs.join(', ')}</li><li>고속 출력: ${S.hs_outputs.join(', ')}</li>`;
  for (const b in S.buses) h += `<li>중요 제어 버스: ${b} (${S.buses[b].bits.length}b) — ${S.buses[b].function}</li>`;
  h += `<li>일반 제어(생략): ${S.general.join(', ')}</li><li>바이어스(생략): ${S.bias.join(', ')}</li></ul>`;
  h += '<h2>가정한 답 (질문)</h2><ul>' + S.questions.map(q => `<li><b>${q.id}</b> ${q.item}: ${q.assumed}</li>`).join('') + '</ul>';
  h += '<h2>모든 화면</h2><ul id="views">' + DATA.views.map(x => `<li><a onclick="go('${x.id}')">${x.title}</a></li>`).join('') + '</ul>';
  sd.innerHTML = h;
  window.scrollTo(0, 0);
}
document.getElementById('back').onclick = () => { const v = location.hash.slice(1); if (DATA.parent[v]) go(DATA.parent[v]); };
document.getElementById('wl').onchange = e => document.body.classList.toggle('nosize', !e.target.checked);
if (PPTX) document.getElementById('dl').href = 'data:application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation;base64,' + PPTX;
else document.getElementById('dl').style.display = 'none';
window.addEventListener('hashchange', () => show(location.hash.slice(1)));
show(location.hash.slice(1) || DATA.views[0].id);
</script></body></html>
'''
page = page.replace('__DATA__', json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)).replace('__PPTX__', pptx_b64)
open(OUT + '/concept.html', 'w').write(page)
print('html bytes', len(page))
````

### FILE: reference/rx_afe/work/make_pptx.py
<!-- sha256=48e47eeaf53d9b86f5bccf0547e8117f2716cb40e669208da6ec7b181542c2c2 newline=1 -->
````python
"""Editable PPTX: one slide per view built from native shapes (freeform lines, ovals, rectangles, text boxes),
click on a block jumps to its child slide, slide notes hold the omitted list. Usage: make_pptx.py <workdir> <outdir>"""
import json, sys, copy
from pptx import Presentation
from pptx.util import Emu, Pt, Inches
from pptx.dml.color import RGBColor
from pptx.enum.text import PP_ALIGN, MSO_ANCHOR
from pptx.enum.shapes import MSO_SHAPE
from pptx.oxml.ns import qn
from lxml import etree

W, OUT = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
scenes = json.load(open(W + '/scenes.json'))
texts = json.load(open(W + '/texts.json'))
ann = json.load(open(OUT + '/annotations.json'))
cls_md = open(OUT + '/classification.md').read()

prs = Presentation()
prs.slide_width = Inches(13.333)
prs.slide_height = Inches(7.5)
blank = prs.slide_layouts[6]
EMU_IN = 914400
FONT = 'Arial'


def rgb(c):
    c = c.lstrip('#')
    if len(c) == 3:
        c = ''.join(ch * 2 for ch in c)
    return RGBColor.from_string(c.upper())


def add_text(slide, x, y, w, h, s, size_pt, color='#111', bold=False, italic=False, align='l', valign='m', wrap=False):
    tb = slide.shapes.add_textbox(Emu(int(x)), Emu(int(y)), Emu(int(max(w, 1))), Emu(int(max(h, 1))))
    tf = tb.text_frame
    tf.margin_left = tf.margin_right = tf.margin_top = tf.margin_bottom = 0
    tf.word_wrap = wrap
    tf.vertical_anchor = {'m': MSO_ANCHOR.MIDDLE, 't': MSO_ANCHOR.TOP}[valign]
    p = tf.paragraphs[0]
    p.alignment = {'l': PP_ALIGN.LEFT, 'c': PP_ALIGN.CENTER, 'r': PP_ALIGN.RIGHT}[align]
    r = p.add_run()
    r.text = s
    f = r.font
    f.size = Pt(size_pt); f.bold = bold; f.italic = italic; f.name = FONT
    f.color.rgb = rgb(color)
    return tb


def no_shadow(shape):
    sp = shape._element.spPr
    etree.SubElement(sp, qn('a:effectLst'))
    st = shape._element.find(qn('p:style'))
    if st is not None:
        shape._element.remove(st)


slides = {}
order = [v['id'] for v in scenes]
# summary slide first
s0 = prs.slides.add_slide(blank)
add_text(s0, Inches(0.4), Inches(0.25), Inches(12), Inches(0.5), 'RX_AFE_TOP — High-Speed IO 개념도 (분류 확인 표)', 22, bold=True, valign='t')
tbl = cls_md.split('```')[1].strip().splitlines()
y = Inches(0.95)
for ln in tbl:
    add_text(s0, Inches(0.4), y, Inches(12.5), Inches(0.3), ln, 10, valign='t', wrap=True)
    y += Inches(0.1) + Inches(0.2) * (1 + len(ln) // 150)
y += Inches(0.1)
add_text(s0, Inches(0.4), y, Inches(12), Inches(0.3), '가정한 답 (질문에 답할 사람이 없어 선택한 답)', 13, bold=True, valign='t'); y += Inches(0.38)
for q in ann['_questions_and_assumed_answers']:
    s = '%s %s: %s' % (q['id'], q['item'], q['assumed'])
    tb = add_text(s0, Inches(0.4), y, Inches(12.5), Inches(0.3), s, 9, valign='t', wrap=True)
    y += Inches(0.42) if len(s) > 170 else Inches(0.27)
s0.notes_slide.notes_text_frame.text = '분류 확인 표와 질문/가정한 답 전문은 classification.md, annotations.json 참조.'

for v in scenes:
    slides[v['id']] = prs.slides.add_slide(blank)

for v in scenes:
    sl = slides[v['id']]
    top = Inches(0.75)
    avail_w, avail_h = prs.slide_width - Inches(0.6), prs.slide_height - top - Inches(0.3)
    sc = min(avail_w / v['w'], avail_h / v['h'])        # EMU per px
    ox = (prs.slide_width - v['w'] * sc) / 2
    oy = top
    X = lambda x: ox + x * sc
    Y = lambda y: oy + y * sc
    pt = lambda px: px * sc / 12700.0                   # px -> points at this scale
    add_text(sl, Inches(0.3), Inches(0.15), Inches(12.5), Inches(0.45), v['title'], 18, bold=True)
    tbox = {t['tid']: t for t in texts.get(v['id'], [])}
    for d in v['draw']:
        if d['t'] in ('line', 'poly'):
            pts = d['pts']
            closed = d['t'] == 'poly'
            fb = sl.shapes.build_freeform(int(X(pts[0][0])), int(Y(pts[0][1])), scale=1.0)
            fb.add_line_segments([(int(X(p[0])), int(Y(p[1]))) for p in pts[1:]], close=closed)
            shp = fb.convert_to_shape()
            if closed:
                if d.get('fill') and d['fill'] != 'none':
                    shp.fill.solid(); shp.fill.fore_color.rgb = rgb(d['fill'])
                else:
                    shp.fill.background()
                if d.get('stroke'):
                    shp.line.color.rgb = rgb(d['stroke']); shp.line.width = Pt(max(pt(d['w']), 0.25))
                else:
                    shp.line.fill.background()
            else:
                shp.fill.background()
                shp.line.color.rgb = rgb(d['c']); shp.line.width = Pt(max(pt(d['w']), 0.25))
            no_shadow(shp)
        elif d['t'] == 'circle':
            r = d['r']
            shp = sl.shapes.add_shape(MSO_SHAPE.OVAL, Emu(int(X(d['cx'] - r))), Emu(int(Y(d['cy'] - r))), Emu(int(2 * r * sc)), Emu(int(2 * r * sc)))
            if d['fill'] != 'none':
                shp.fill.solid(); shp.fill.fore_color.rgb = rgb(d['fill'])
            else:
                shp.fill.background()
            shp.line.color.rgb = rgb(d['stroke']); shp.line.width = Pt(max(pt(d['w']), 0.25))
            no_shadow(shp)
        elif d['t'] == 'rect':
            shp = sl.shapes.add_shape(MSO_SHAPE.RECTANGLE, Emu(int(X(d['x']))), Emu(int(Y(d['y']))), Emu(int(d['w'] * sc)), Emu(int(d['h'] * sc)))
            if d['fill'] != 'none':
                shp.fill.solid(); shp.fill.fore_color.rgb = rgb(d['fill'])
            else:
                shp.fill.background()
            shp.line.color.rgb = rgb(d['stroke']); shp.line.width = Pt(max(pt(d['sw']), 0.25))
            no_shadow(shp)
    # texts after shapes (on top)
    for d in v['draw']:
        if d['t'] != 'text':
            continue
        b = tbox.get(d['tid'])
        wpx = (b['x1'] - b['x0']) if b else len(d['s']) * d['size'] * 0.6
        wpx += 6
        hpx = d['size'] * 1.4
        if d['anchor'] == 'start':
            x0, al = d['x'], 'l'
        elif d['anchor'] == 'end':
            x0, al = d['x'] - wpx, 'r'
        else:
            x0, al = d['x'] - wpx / 2, 'c'
        add_text(sl, X(x0), Y(d['y'] - hpx / 2), wpx * sc, hpx * sc, d['s'], pt(d['size']),
                 color=d['color'], bold=d['weight'] == 'bold', italic=d['italic'], align=al)
    # clickable hotspots -> child slide
    for h in v['hot']:
        if h['link'] not in slides:
            continue
        shp = sl.shapes.add_shape(MSO_SHAPE.RECTANGLE, Emu(int(X(h['x']))), Emu(int(Y(h['y']))), Emu(int(h['w'] * sc)), Emu(int(h['h'] * sc)))
        shp.fill.solid(); shp.fill.fore_color.rgb = RGBColor(0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF)
        sf = shp._element.spPr.find(qn('a:solidFill'))
        clr = sf.find(qn('a:srgbClr'))
        a = etree.SubElement(clr, qn('a:alpha')); a.set('val', '0')
        shp.line.fill.background()
        no_shadow(shp)
        shp.click_action.target_slide = slides[h['link']]
        shp.name = 'link to ' + h['link']
    # back link to parent
    parents = [p for p in scenes if any(h['link'] == v['id'] for h in p['hot'])]
    if parents:
        tb = add_text(sl, Inches(11.6), Inches(0.2), Inches(1.5), Inches(0.35), '▲ 위로 (%s)' % parents[0]['cell'], 10, color='#1d5fbf', align='r')
        tb.click_action.target_slide = slides[parents[0]['id']]
    # notes: omitted list
    lines = ['생략 목록 (이 화면):']
    for o in v['omitted']:
        lines.append(('- %s — %s' % (o['net'], o['class'])) if 'net' in o else ('- %s (%s) — %s' % (o['block'], o['cell'], o['reason'])))
    if not v['omitted']:
        lines.append('- 없음')
    if v['notes']:
        lines.append('주석:')
        lines += ['- ' + n for n in v['notes']]
    lines.append('클릭 가능한 블록 → 하위 화면: ' + ', '.join(sorted({h['link'] for h in v['hot']})) if v['hot'] else '하위 화면 없음')
    sl.notes_slide.notes_text_frame.text = '\n'.join(lines)

prs.save(OUT + '/concept.pptx')
print('slides', len(prs.slides))
````

### FILE: reference/rx_afe/work/measure.js
<!-- sha256=0fa8be3455b0813b8688239633226821f434784bf777ff3c60e35428d76be3aa newline=1 -->
````javascript
// measure text boxes of every view (for the overlap checker) and take one screenshot per view
// usage: node measure.js <concept.html> <texts.json> <screenshot dir> [mobile]
const { chromium } = require('playwright');
const fs = require('fs');
(async () => {
  const [file, outJson, shotDir, mobile] = process.argv.slice(2);
  const browser = await chromium.launch();
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: mobile ? { width: 390, height: 844 } : { width: 1820, height: 1000 }, deviceScaleFactor: 1 });
  await page.goto('file://' + file);
  const ids = await page.evaluate(() => DATA.views.map(v => v.id));
  const res = {};
  for (const id of ids) {
    await page.evaluate(i => { location.hash = i; }, id);
    await page.waitForTimeout(120);
    res[id] = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const out = [];
      document.querySelectorAll('#stage svg text').forEach(t => {
        const b = t.getBBox();
        out.push({ tid: t.getAttribute('data-tid'), s: t.textContent, x0: b.x, y0: b.y, x1: b.x + b.width, y1: b.y + b.height, cls: t.getAttribute('class') });
      });
      return out;
    });
    if (mobile) { const ov = await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth - window.innerWidth); if (ov > 0) console.log('OVERFLOW', id, ov); }
    if (shotDir) await page.screenshot({ path: `${shotDir}/${id}${mobile ? '_mobile' : ''}.png`, fullPage: true });
  }
  if (outJson) fs.writeFileSync(outJson, JSON.stringify(res));
  // functional check: clicking a hotspot navigates down one level
  await page.evaluate(() => { location.hash = 'b_top'; });
  await page.waitForTimeout(100);
  const before = await page.evaluate(() => location.hash);
  const hot = await page.$('#stage svg rect.hot');
  if (hot) { await hot.click(); await page.waitForTimeout(100); }
  const after = await page.evaluate(() => location.hash);
  await page.evaluate(() => { document.getElementById('wl').click(); });
  const hidden = await page.evaluate(() => getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('#stage svg text.size') || document.body).display);
  const sw = await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth - window.innerWidth);
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ click_from: before, click_to: after, size_text_display_after_toggle: hidden, horizontal_overflow_px: sw }));
  await browser.close();
})();
````

### FILE: reference/rx_afe/work/verify.py
<!-- sha256=ae5d156944190a0b14c93abd372ebddab7a265ed3f6623ce1804e2ef3ce2896c newline=1 -->
````python
"""Plan section 6: verification.
Re-reads connectivity ONLY from drawn geometry (wire polylines, pin coordinates, junction dots, labels/ports/power
symbols) and compares it with the netlist; then runs the quality checks.
Usage: verify.py <workdir> <netlist> <out verify_report.json>"""
import json, sys, re, math, itertools, collections
from netlist import parse

W, NL, OUTF = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
cells, order = parse(NL)
A = json.load(open(W + '/analysis.json'))
CN = A['cellnet']
scenes = json.load(open(W + '/scenes.json'))
texts = json.load(open(W + '/texts.json'))
EPS = 0.6
HIDDEN = {'general_control', 'bias', 'power', 'unused', 'internal'}


def expand(name):
    groups = list(re.finditer(r'<(\d+):(\d+)>', name))
    if not groups:
        return [name]
    parts = []; last = 0; ranges = []
    for g in groups:
        parts.append(name[last:g.start()]); last = g.end()
        a, b = int(g.group(1)), int(g.group(2))
        ranges.append(list(range(a, b + 1)) if a <= b else list(range(a, b - 1, -1)))
    parts.append(name[last:])
    out = []
    for combo in itertools.product(*ranges):
        s = parts[0]
        for k, v in enumerate(combo):
            s += str(v) + parts[k + 1]
        out.append(s)
    return out


def collapse(vec):
    vec = tuple(vec)
    return (vec[0],) if len(set(vec)) == 1 else vec


def segs(pts):
    return [(pts[i], pts[i + 1]) for i in range(len(pts) - 1)]


def on_seg(p, a, b, tol=EPS):
    (x, y), (x1, y1), (x2, y2) = p, a, b
    dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
    L2 = dx * dx + dy * dy
    if L2 == 0:
        return math.hypot(x - x1, y - y1) <= tol
    t = ((x - x1) * dx + (y - y1) * dy) / L2
    if t < -1e-9 or t > 1 + 1e-9:
        return False
    px, py = x1 + t * dx, y1 + t * dy
    return math.hypot(x - px, y - py) <= tol


def same(p, q, tol=EPS):
    return abs(p[0] - q[0]) <= tol and abs(p[1] - q[1]) <= tol


def seg_intersection(a, b, c, d):
    """proper intersection point of segments ab and cd (None if parallel/none)"""
    (x1, y1), (x2, y2), (x3, y3), (x4, y4) = a, b, c, d
    den = (x1 - x2) * (y3 - y4) - (y1 - y2) * (x3 - x4)
    if abs(den) < 1e-9:
        return None
    t = ((x1 - x3) * (y3 - y4) - (y1 - y3) * (x3 - x4)) / den
    u = -((x1 - x2) * (y1 - y3) - (y1 - y2) * (x1 - x3)) / den
    if 0 <= t <= 1 and 0 <= u <= 1:
        return (x1 + t * (x2 - x1), y1 + t * (y2 - y1)), t, u
    return None


def seg_hits_box(a, b, box, shrink=1.0):
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = box[0] + shrink, box[1] + shrink, box[2] - shrink, box[3] - shrink
    if x0 >= x1 or y0 >= y1:
        return False
    # Liang-Barsky clip
    (ax, ay), (bx, by) = a, b
    dx, dy = bx - ax, by - ay
    p = [-dx, dx, -dy, dy]; q = [ax - x0, x1 - ax, ay - y0, y1 - ay]
    u0, u1 = 0.0, 1.0
    for pi, qi in zip(p, q):
        if abs(pi) < 1e-12:
            if qi < 0:
                return False
        else:
            t = qi / pi
            if pi < 0:
                u0 = max(u0, t)
            else:
                u1 = min(u1, t)
    return u1 - u0 > 1e-6


class UF:
    def __init__(self):
        self.p = {}
    def f(self, x):
        self.p.setdefault(x, x)
        while self.p[x] != x:
            self.p[x] = self.p[self.p[x]]; x = self.p[x]
        return x
    def u(self, a, b):
        self.p[self.f(a)] = self.f(b)


def local_net(cell, elem, term):
    for e in cells[cell]['elems']:
        if e['name'] == elem:
            if e['kind'] == 'inst':
                return e['pins'][term]
            return e['terms'][term]
    raise KeyError(elem)


def elem_of(cell, name):
    for e in cells[cell]['elems']:
        if e['name'] == name:
            return e
    return None


def check_view(v):
    cell = v['cell']
    cn = CN[cell]
    errs = []
    info = {}
    wires = v['wires']
    syms = [s for s in v['symbols'] if s.get('pins') is not None]
    pins = []
    for s in syms:
        for p in s.get('pins', []):
            pins.append((s, p))
    dots = [tuple(d) for d in v['dots']]
    labels = v['labels']
    # ---------------- 1. geometry -> nets ----------------
    uf = UF()
    W_ = ['w%d' % i for i in range(len(wires))]
    for i in range(len(wires)):
        uf.f(W_[i])
    # wire-wire connections (endpoint on other wire / shared vertex)
    junction_pts = collections.defaultdict(lambda: 0)
    for i, w in enumerate(wires):
        for end in (w['pts'][0], w['pts'][-1]):
            for j, w2 in enumerate(wires):
                if j == i:
                    continue
                for a, b in segs(w2['pts']):
                    if on_seg(end, a, b):
                        uf.u(W_[i], W_[j]); break
    # branch count at every candidate point
    cand = set()
    for w in wires:
        for p in w['pts']:
            cand.add((round(p[0], 1), round(p[1], 1)))
    for s, p in pins:
        cand.add((round(p['x'], 1), round(p['y'], 1)))
    for d in dots:
        cand.add((round(d[0], 1), round(d[1], 1)))
    def branches(P):
        n = 0
        for w in wires:
            pts = w['pts']
            for k, q in enumerate(pts):
                if same(P, q):
                    n += 1 if k in (0, len(pts) - 1) else 2
                    break
            else:
                for a, b in segs(pts):
                    if on_seg(P, a, b):
                        n += 2; break
        for s, p in pins:
            if same(P, (p['x'], p['y'])):
                n += 1
        return n
    missing_dot, extra_dot = [], []
    for P in cand:
        br = branches(P)
        has = any(same(P, d) for d in dots)
        if br >= 3 and not has:
            # a pin sitting exactly at the corner of its own lead counts as 2-branch: ignore pin+wire end only
            missing_dot.append(P)
        if has and br < 3:
            extra_dot.append(P)
    for d in dots:
        if branches(d) < 3 and d not in extra_dot:
            extra_dot.append(d)
    # pins -> wires
    pinnode = {}
    open_pins = []
    pin_mid = []
    for idx, (s, p) in enumerate(pins):
        key = 'p%d' % idx
        pinnode[idx] = key
        uf.f(key)
        hit = False
        for i, w in enumerate(wires):
            if same((p['x'], p['y']), w['pts'][0]) or same((p['x'], p['y']), w['pts'][-1]):
                uf.u(key, W_[i]); hit = True
            else:
                for a, b in segs(w['pts']):
                    if on_seg((p['x'], p['y']), a, b):
                        pin_mid.append((s['id'], p['name'])); uf.u(key, W_[i]); hit = True; break
        # pin touching another pin directly
        for jdx, (s2, p2) in enumerate(pins):
            if jdx != idx and same((p['x'], p['y']), (p2['x'], p2['y'])):
                uf.u(key, 'p%d' % jdx); hit = True
        if not hit:
            open_pins.append(idx)
    # names
    names = collections.defaultdict(set)   # root -> names
    label_unattached = []
    gray_on = {}
    for lb in labels:
        at = lb['at']
        attached = None
        for i, w in enumerate(wires):
            if any(on_seg(at, a, b) for a, b in segs(w['pts'])):
                attached = W_[i]; break
        if attached is None:
            for idx, (s, p) in enumerate(pins):
                if same(at, (p['x'], p['y'])):
                    attached = pinnode[idx]
                    if lb['kind'] == 'gray':
                        gray_on[idx] = lb['name']
                    break
        if attached is None:
            label_unattached.append(lb['name'])
            continue
        lb['_node'] = attached
    # merge by name
    byname = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for lb in labels:
        if '_node' in lb:
            nm = lb['name']
            byname[nm].append(lb['_node'])
    for nm, nodes in byname.items():
        for n in nodes[1:]:
            uf.u(nodes[0], n)
    for lb in labels:
        if '_node' in lb:
            names[uf.f(lb['_node'])].add(lb['name'])
    # open wire ends
    open_ends = []
    for i, w in enumerate(wires):
        for end in (w['pts'][0], w['pts'][-1]):
            ok = False
            for j, w2 in enumerate(wires):
                if j != i and any(on_seg(end, a, b) for a, b in segs(w2['pts'])):
                    ok = True; break
            if not ok:
                ok = any(same(end, (p['x'], p['y'])) for s, p in pins)
            if not ok:
                ok = any(on_seg(lb['at'], end, end, tol=EPS) or same(lb['at'], end) for lb in labels) or \
                     any(on_seg(tuple(lb['at']), w['pts'][0], w['pts'][-1]) and lb['kind'] in ('net', 'port') for lb in labels if '_node' in lb and lb['_node'] == W_[i])
            if not ok and w['cls'] == 'power' and any(lb['kind'] == 'power' and '_node' in lb and uf.f(lb['_node']) == uf.f(W_[i]) for lb in labels):
                ok = True      # supply rail drawn as a long bar
            if not ok:
                open_ends.append(end)
    # ---------------- 2. expected nets from the netlist ----------------
    pin_vec = {}
    decl_err = []
    for idx, (s, p) in enumerate(pins):
        vec = []
        for sl in p['terms']:
            nets = {local_net(cell, e, t) for e, t in sl}
            if len(nets) != 1:
                decl_err.append('%s.%s slice %s -> %s' % (s['id'], p['name'], sl, sorted(nets)))
            vec.append(sorted(nets)[0])
        pin_vec[idx] = collapse(vec)
    groups = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for idx in pin_vec:
        groups[uf.f(pinnode[idx])].append(idx)
    mismatch = []
    for root, idxs in groups.items():
        vecs = {pin_vec[i] for i in idxs}
        nm = names.get(root, set())
        if len(vecs) > 1:
            mismatch.append({'net_pins': ['%s.%s' % (pins[i][0]['id'], pins[i][1]['name']) for i in idxs], 'netlist': sorted(map(list, vecs))})
        for n in nm:
            ex = expand(n)
            rg = [(int(x), int(y)) for x, y in re.findall(r'<(\d+):(\d+)>', n)]
            if rg and all(x > y for x, y in rg):
                ex = ex[::-1]          # names are written <MSB:LSB>; slice vectors are listed LSB first
            ev = collapse(ex)
            for vv in vecs:
                if ev != vv:
                    mismatch.append({'name': n, 'pins': ['%s.%s' % (pins[i][0]['id'], pins[i][1]['name']) for i in idxs], 'netlist': list(vv)})
    # named nets without pins: name must be a net of the cell
    for root, nm in names.items():
        if root not in groups:
            for n in nm:
                for x in expand(n):
                    if x not in cn:
                        mismatch.append({'name': n, 'error': 'label names a net that does not exist in the cell'})
    # completeness: a netlist net must not be split into two drawn nets
    where = collections.defaultdict(set)
    for root, idxs in groups.items():
        for i in idxs:
            where[pin_vec[i]].add(root)
    split = {str(list(k)): len(r) for k, r in where.items() if len(r) > 1}
    # open pins: only allowed with a gray label (hidden net)
    bad_open = []
    for idx in open_pins:
        s, p = pins[idx]
        nm = names.get(uf.f(pinnode[idx]), set())
        if not nm:
            bad_open.append('%s.%s' % (s['id'], p['name']))
    # gray labels only on hidden nets
    gray_bad = [n for i, n in gray_on.items() if not (set(cn.get(n, [])) & HIDDEN)]
    # ---------------- 3. coverage / hidden pins / omitted list / bundles ----------------
    scope = v.get('scope')
    elems = [e for e in cells[cell]['elems']]
    if scope:
        elems = [e for e in elems if e['name'] in scope]
    covered = collections.Counter()
    for s in v['symbols']:
        for m in s.get('members', []):
            covered[m] += 1
    omitted_blocks = {o['block'] for o in v['omitted'] if 'block' in o}
    uncovered = [e['name'] for e in elems if covered[e['name']] == 0 and e['name'] not in omitted_blocks]
    double = [m for m, c in covered.items() if c > 1]
    # drawn terminals per element
    drawn = collections.defaultdict(set)
    for idx, (s, p) in enumerate(pins):
        for sl in p['terms']:
            for e, t in sl:
                drawn[e].add(t)
    hidden_bad = []
    bundle_bad = []
    for s in v['symbols']:
        mem = s.get('members', [])
        if not mem:
            continue
        for m in mem:
            e = elem_of(cell, m)
            if e is None:
                continue
            terms = e['pins'] if e['kind'] == 'inst' else {k: n for k, n in e['terms'].items() if not (e['kind'] == 'mos' and k == 'b')}
            for t, n in terms.items():
                if t in drawn[m]:
                    continue
                cls = set(cn.get(n, []))
                # internal to the symbol's bundle?
                users = set()
                for f in cells[cell]['elems']:
                    vals = f['pins'].values() if f['kind'] == 'inst' else [x for k, x in f['terms'].items() if not (f['kind'] == 'mos' and k == 'b')]
                    if n in vals:
                        users.add(f['name'])
                if n in cells[cell]['ports'] and not (cls & HIDDEN):
                    hidden_bad.append('%s.%s=%s (%s) not drawn' % (m, t, n, '/'.join(sorted(cls))))
                elif users <= set(mem):
                    continue      # internal net of the bundle / group
                elif cls & {'data', 'clock', 'important_control'}:
                    hidden_bad.append('%s.%s=%s (%s) not drawn' % (m, t, n, '/'.join(sorted(cls))))
                elif not (cls & HIDDEN):
                    hidden_bad.append('%s.%s=%s (%s) not drawn' % (m, t, n, '/'.join(sorted(cls))))
                elif 'internal' in cls and len(mem) > 1:
                    bundle_bad.append('%s: internal net %s used outside the bundle by %s' % (s['id'], n, sorted(users - set(mem))))
    # visible ports of the cell present as port glyphs?
    portnames = set()
    for lb in labels:
        if lb['kind'] == 'port':
            portnames |= set(expand(lb['name']))
    vis_ports = [p for p in cells[cell]['ports'] if set(cn.get(p, [])) & {'data', 'clock', 'important_control'}]
    if scope:
        sc_nets = set()
        for e in elems:
            sc_nets |= set(e['pins'].values() if e['kind'] == 'inst' else e['terms'].values())
        vis_ports = [p for p in vis_ports if p in sc_nets]
    missing_ports = [p for p in vis_ports if p not in portnames]
    hidden_ports_drawn = [p for p in portnames if p in cn and not (set(cn[p]) & {'data', 'clock', 'important_control'})]
    # omitted list = hidden classification (block views)
    om_nets = {o['net'] for o in v['omitted'] if 'net' in o}
    drawn_nets = set()
    for idx in pin_vec:
        drawn_nets |= set(pin_vec[idx])
    for lb in labels:
        drawn_nets |= set(expand(lb['name']))
    om_bad = []
    group_internal = set()
    for s in v['symbols']:
        mem = set(s.get('members', []))
        if len(mem) > 1:
            for f in cells[cell]['elems']:
                if f['name'] in mem:
                    for n in (f['pins'].values() if f['kind'] == 'inst' else f['terms'].values()):
                        users = {g['name'] for g in cells[cell]['elems'] if n in (g['pins'].values() if g['kind'] == 'inst' else g['terms'].values())}
                        if users <= mem and n not in cells[cell]['ports']:
                            group_internal.add(n)
    if v['level'] == 'block':
        for n, c in cn.items():
            if n in drawn_nets or n in group_internal:
                continue
            if set(c) & {'data', 'clock', 'important_control'}:
                om_bad.append('visible net %s not drawn' % n)
            elif n not in om_nets and not (set(c) <= {'internal'}):
                om_bad.append('hidden net %s missing from omitted list' % n)
    conn_errors = {
        'declaration_errors': decl_err, 'net_mismatch': mismatch, 'split_nets': split, 'open_pins': bad_open,
        'open_wire_ends': [list(map(lambda z: round(z, 1), e)) for e in open_ends], 'pin_on_wire_interior': pin_mid,
        'missing_junction_dot': [list(p) for p in missing_dot], 'superfluous_dot': [list(p) for p in extra_dot],
        'unattached_labels': label_unattached, 'gray_label_on_visible_net': gray_bad,
        'uncovered_elements': uncovered, 'elements_drawn_twice': double, 'visible_terminal_hidden': hidden_bad,
        'bundle_internal_net_used_outside': bundle_bad, 'missing_ports': missing_ports, 'hidden_ports_drawn': hidden_ports_drawn,
        'omitted_list_errors': om_bad,
    }
    n_err = sum(len(x) for x in conn_errors.values())
    # ---------------- 4. quality ----------------
    allsegs = []
    for i, w in enumerate(wires):
        for a, b in segs(w['pts']):
            allsegs.append((i, a, b))
    # crossings
    crossings = []
    for (i, a, b), (j, c, d) in itertools.combinations(allsegs, 2):
        if i == j:
            continue
        r = seg_intersection(a, b, c, d)
        if not r:
            continue
        P, t, u = r
        if 1e-6 < t < 1 - 1e-6 and 1e-6 < u < 1 - 1e-6:
            if any(same(P, dd, 1.0) for dd in dots):
                continue
            crossings.append(P)
    allowed = []
    avoidable = []
    for P in crossings:
        m = [a for a in v['allowed_cross'] if math.hypot(P[0] - a['at'][0], P[1] - a['at'][1]) <= 12]
        if m:
            allowed.append({'at': [round(P[0], 1), round(P[1], 1)], 'reason': m[0]['reason']})
        else:
            avoidable.append([round(P[0], 1), round(P[1], 1)])
    # wires through symbols
    through = []
    for s in v['symbols']:
        for bx in s.get('body', []):
            for (i, a, b) in allsegs:
                if seg_hits_box(a, b, bx, shrink=1.0):
                    through.append('%s by wire %d' % (s['id'], i))
    # twisted lines
    twisted = []
    for i, w in enumerate(wires):
        pts = w['pts']
        L = sum(math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) for a, b in segs(pts))
        man = abs(pts[-1][0] - pts[0][0]) + abs(pts[-1][1] - pts[0][1])
        if man > 0 and L / man > 1.5 + 1e-9:
            twisted.append({'wire': i, 'ratio': round(L / man, 2)})
    # short jogs
    jogs = []
    for i, w in enumerate(wires):
        if len(w['pts']) >= 3:
            for a, b in segs(w['pts']):
                if math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) < 20 - 1e-6:
                    jogs.append({'wire': i, 'len': round(math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]), 1)})
    # close junctions
    close = []
    for p, q in itertools.combinations(dots, 2):
        if math.hypot(p[0] - q[0], p[1] - q[1]) < 20 - 1e-6:
            close.append([list(p), list(q)])
    # text overlap (measured bboxes)
    tb = {t['tid']: t for t in texts.get(v['id'], [])}
    tlist = [t for t in tb.values()]
    overlaps = []
    for a, b in itertools.combinations(tlist, 2):
        ix = min(a['x1'], b['x1']) - max(a['x0'], b['x0'])
        iy = min(a['y1'], b['y1']) - max(a['y0'], b['y0'])
        if ix > 0.5 and iy > 0.5:
            overlaps.append('"%s" x "%s"' % (a['s'], b['s']))
    # text vs lines (wires + symbol strokes + port glyph edges + rect borders)
    strokes = []
    for d in v['draw']:
        if d['t'] == 'line':
            for a, b in segs(d['pts']):
                strokes.append((a, b, d.get('owner')))
        elif d['t'] == 'poly' and d.get('owner') == 'port':
            pts = d['pts'] + [d['pts'][0]]
            for a, b in segs(pts):
                strokes.append((a, b, 'port'))
        elif d['t'] == 'rect':
            x, y, w_, h_ = d['x'], d['y'], d['w'], d['h']
            pts = [(x, y), (x + w_, y), (x + w_, y + h_), (x, y + h_), (x, y)]
            for a, b in segs(pts):
                strokes.append((a, b, d.get('owner')))
        elif d['t'] == 'circle' and d.get('owner') not in ('dot',):
            r = d['r']
            strokes.append(((d['cx'] - r, d['cy']), (d['cx'] + r, d['cy']), d.get('owner')))
    text_line = []
    for t in tlist:
        box_ = [t['x0'], t['y0'], t['x1'], t['y1']]
        for a, b, own in strokes:
            if seg_hits_box(a, b, box_, shrink=1.5):
                text_line.append('"%s" x line(%s)' % (t['s'], own))
                break
    # symmetry
    sym_v = []
    symd = {s['id']: s for s in v['symbols']}
    for g in v['symmetry']:
        ax = g['axis']
        for a, b in g['pairs']:
            sa, sb = symd.get(a), symd.get(b)
            if not sa or not sb:
                sym_v.append('pair %s/%s missing' % (a, b)); continue
            ia, ib = sa['info'], sb['info']
            if g['orient'] == 'v':
                if 'cx' in ia:
                    ok = abs((ia['cx'] + ib['cx']) / 2 - ax) < 0.6 and abs(ia['cy'] - ib['cy']) < 0.6 and ia['gate'] != ib['gate'] and sa['kind'] == sb['kind']
                else:
                    ok = abs((ia['x'] + ib['x']) / 2 - ax) < 0.6 and abs(ia['y'] - ib['y']) < 0.6
                if not ok:
                    sym_v.append('pair %s/%s not mirrored about x=%s' % (a, b, ax))
            else:
                ok = abs((ia['y0'] + ia['h'] / 2 + ib['y0'] + ib['h'] / 2) / 2 - ax) < 0.6 and abs(ia['x0'] - ib['x0']) < 0.6
                if not ok:
                    sym_v.append('pair %s/%s not mirrored about y=%s' % (a, b, ax))
        for a in g['self']:
            sa = symd.get(a)
            if not sa:
                continue
            ia = sa['info']
            cx = ia.get('cx', ia.get('x'))
            if abs(cx - ax) > 0.6:
                sym_v.append('%s not on axis' % a)
        # wire mirror check
        if g['orient'] == 'v':
            ymin = g.get('ymin', -1e9)
            ex = g.get('exempt_boxes', [])
            def clip(a, b):
                if a[1] < ymin and b[1] < ymin:
                    return None
                if a[1] < ymin:
                    a = (a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * (ymin - a[1]) / (b[1] - a[1]), ymin)
                if b[1] < ymin:
                    b = (b[0] + (a[0] - b[0]) * (ymin - b[1]) / (a[1] - b[1]), ymin)
                return a, b
            selfgate = [(p['x'], p['y']) for a in g['self'] if a in symd for p in symd[a]['pins'] if p['name'] == 'G']
            insegs = []
            for (i, a, b) in allsegs:
                if any(same(a, q) or same(b, q) for q in selfgate):
                    continue
                if min(a[0], b[0]) >= g['x0'] - 0.1 and max(a[0], b[0]) <= g['x1'] + 0.1:
                    c = clip(a, b)
                    if c and math.hypot(c[1][0] - c[0][0], c[1][1] - c[0][1]) > 1:
                        if any(seg_hits_box(c[0], c[1], bx, shrink=0) or (bx[0] <= c[0][0] <= bx[2] and bx[1] <= c[0][1] <= bx[3]) for bx in ex):
                            continue
                        insegs.append(c)
            def covered_by(a, b):
                # mirrored segment must lie on the union of in-region segments (sample points)
                for k in range(11):
                    t = k / 10
                    P = (a[0] + t * (b[0] - a[0]), a[1] + t * (b[1] - a[1]))
                    if not any(on_seg(P, c, d, tol=0.8) for c, d in insegs) and not any(on_seg(P, c, d, tol=0.8) for _, c, d in allsegs):
                        return False
                return True
            unmatched = 0
            for a, b in insegs:
                ma = (2 * ax - a[0], a[1]); mb = (2 * ax - b[0], b[1])
                if not covered_by(ma, mb):
                    unmatched += 1
            if unmatched:
                sym_v.append('%d wire segment(s) without mirror image about x=%s' % (unmatched, ax))
        else:
            for (i, a, b) in allsegs:
                if min(a[0], b[0]) >= g['x0'] and max(a[0], b[0]) <= g['x1']:
                    ma = (a[0], 2 * ax - a[1]); mb = (b[0], 2 * ax - b[1])
                    ok = all(any(on_seg((ma[0] + t / 10 * (mb[0] - ma[0]), ma[1] + t / 10 * (mb[1] - ma[1])), c, d, tol=0.8) for _, c, d in allsegs) for t in range(11))
                    if not ok:
                        sym_v.append('wire %d has no mirror image about y=%s' % (i, ax))
    quality = {
        'text_overlap': len(overlaps) + len(text_line), 'text_text_overlap': overlaps, 'text_line_overlap': text_line,
        'wire_through_symbol': through, 'symmetry_violations': sym_v, 'twisted_lines': twisted, 'short_jogs': jogs,
        'close_junctions': close, 'avoidable_crossings': avoidable, 'structural_crossings': allowed,
    }
    counts = {'text_overlap': len(overlaps) + len(text_line), 'wire_through_symbol': len(through), 'symmetry_violations': len(sym_v),
              'twisted_lines': len(twisted), 'short_jogs': len(jogs), 'close_junctions': len(close),
              'avoidable_crossings': len(avoidable), 'structural_crossings_allowed': len(allowed)}
    return {'view': v['id'], 'cell': cell, 'level': v['level'], 'title': v['title'],
            'connectivity': {'pass': n_err == 0, 'error_count': n_err, 'errors': {k: x for k, x in conn_errors.items() if x},
                             'drawn_nets': len(groups), 'drawn_pins': len(pins)},
            'quality_counts': counts, 'quality_details': {k: x for k, x in quality.items() if x and k != 'text_overlap'},
            'quality_pass': all(counts[k] == 0 for k in counts if k != 'structural_crossings_allowed')}


rep = [check_view(v) for v in scenes]
summary = {'views': len(rep), 'connectivity_pass': sum(r['connectivity']['pass'] for r in rep),
           'quality_pass': sum(r['quality_pass'] for r in rep)}
json.dump({'summary': summary, 'views': rep}, open(OUTF, 'w'), indent=1, ensure_ascii=False)
for r in rep:
    c = r['connectivity']
    print('%-12s conn=%s err=%d  %s' % (r['view'], 'PASS' if c['pass'] else 'FAIL', c['error_count'],
                                        {k: v for k, v in r['quality_counts'].items() if v}))
    if not c['pass']:
        for k, x in c['errors'].items():
            print('     ', k, str(x)[:600])
print(summary)
````

### FILE: reference/rx_afe/expected/classification.md
<!-- sha256=a9dd4e98774c6d1a250d3a6b6963cba785f023410bbf089a2c26a45fcead9973 newline=1 -->
````markdown
# 분류 확인 표 — RX_AFE_TOP

```
고속 데이터 입력 : RX_INP, RX_INN                           (확신 높음 — 사용자 지정, 구조 일치: CTLE 입력쌍 게이트 + 종단/ESD 패드)
클럭 입력        : CLK_INP, CLK_INN                         (확신 높음 — 구조: latch 클럭 스티어링·StrongARM 꼬리/리셋 게이트까지 도달, 이름 일치)
고속 출력        : DOUT_EVEN_P, DOUT_EVEN_N, DOUT_ODD_P, DOUT_ODD_N
중요 제어 (버스) : W<4:1>_<3:0> (16b) — DFE 탭1~4 계수 (IDAC 스위치 → 탭 쌍 꼬리 전류, 탭 입력 = 귀환 데이터, summer 출력에서 합산)  (확신 높음; 묶음 방식은 Q7)
생략             : 제어 10개 (C0, C1, C2, R0, R1, R2, G0, G1, G2, G3), 바이어스 4개 (IREF, VBN, VBP, VCM_RX), 전원 2개 (VDD, VSS), 블록 1개 (X_BIAS (BIAS_GEN: bias))
질문             : Q1 C0, C1, C2 (CTLE_STAGE) / Q2 X_DIV_M / X_DIV_S in CLK_DIST_TREE / Q3 M_TIE_P/M_TIE_P2, M_TIE_N/M_TIE_N2 / Q4 STRONGARM_SLICER: M11 / Q5 NAND2 (static_cmos_gate), SR_LATCH (cross_coupled_gates) / Q6 top: CLKQ, CLKQB; CLK_DIST_TREE: CLKI_BUF, CLKIB_BUF, CLKQ_BUF, CLKQB_BUF; STRONGARM_SLICER: QPB, QNB / Q7 W<4:1>_<3:0>
```

## 질문과 가정한 답 (실행 중 답할 사람이 없어 가장 그럴듯한 답을 선택하여 annotations.json에 저장)

- **Q1** `C0, C1, C2 (CTLE_STAGE)` — 이름(C*)은 커패시터 뱅크 제어처럼 보이지만, 구조상 스위치 MC*P/MC*N은 SP-C*_MID-SN 직렬 경로(저항 경로)이고 C*_CAP은 SP-SN에 항상 연결된 고정 C입니다. 구조대로 그릴까요? (예: 가변 R + 고정 C / 아니오: 가변 C로)
  - 확신: low (이름-구조 불일치)
  - 가정한 답: 예 — 구조를 따름. C0..C2 스위치는 가변 R 묶음에 포함, C_DEG_BASE+C0_CAP+C1_CAP+C2_CAP은 고정 C 하나(합계 330f)로 그림. C0..C2는 일반 제어(그리지 않음).
- **Q2** `X_DIV_M / X_DIV_S in CLK_DIST_TREE` — 인스턴스 이름은 ÷2 분주기(DIV)이지만 구조상 귀환 루프가 없습니다 (master의 D 입력 = 자기 클럭 B1N/B1P, slave 출력은 어디로도 되돌아가지 않음). 구조대로 "LATCH 2개 직렬"로 그릴까요? (예 / ÷2로 그림 / netlist 오류)
  - 확신: low (이름-구조 불일치, 사전의 ÷2 = latch 루프와 불일치)
  - 가정한 답: 예 — 구조대로 LATCH 박스 2개와 실제 연결을 그림. ÷2 기호는 쓰지 않음. netlist 오류 가능성을 보고서에 남김.
- **Q3** `M_TIE_P/M_TIE_P2, M_TIE_N/M_TIE_N2` — 전달 게이트(NMOS 게이트=VSS, PMOS 게이트=VDD)라 항상 꺼져 있습니다. 그러면 O_DOUTP/N(= X_DFE_EVEN의 탭1 귀환 입력 T1_FB)은 떠 있습니다. 의도된 것입니까? (의도됨 / 연결(항상 ON)이어야 함)
  - 확신: low (구조상 항상 OFF)
  - 가정한 답: 끊긴 것으로 처리(계획서 §3.2): TG는 열린 스위치 기호 + "(off)". O_DOUTP/N은 구동이 없지만 X_DFE_EVEN의 고속 핀(T1_FB)에 붙어 있어 고속 데이터로 그리고 이름표로 끝냄.
- **Q4** `STRONGARM_SLICER: M11` — STRONGARM_SLICER에 거울 짝이 없는 소자가 있습니다 (M11: PMOS, S=VDD, D=DP, G=DN; 반대쪽 D=DN, G=DP 소자 없음). 차동 회로가 비대칭이 됩니다. 의도된 것입니까? (의도됨 / netlist 오류)
  - 확신: low (대칭 위반이 netlist에 있음)
  - 가정한 답: 그대로 그림 (정확성 > 대칭, 계획서 §5.2-7). M11은 연결된 노드(DP) 쪽 바깥에 두고, 게이트(DN)는 이름표로 연결, "비대칭" 표시.
- **Q5** `NAND2 (static_cmos_gate), SR_LATCH (cross_coupled_gates)` — 블록 역할 사전에 없는 구조입니다 (정적 CMOS 게이트, 교차 결합 게이트 = SR latch). 박스(셀 이름)로 그릴까요? (예 / 역할 지정)
  - 확신: low (사전에 없음)
  - 가정한 답: 예 — 사전 규칙대로 박스(셀 이름) + 보이는 핀. 유닛 레벨은 소자 그대로.
- **Q6** `top: CLKQ, CLKQB; CLK_DIST_TREE: CLKI_BUF, CLKIB_BUF, CLKQ_BUF, CLKQB_BUF; STRONGARM_SLICER: QPB, QNB` — 추적상 고속 넷이지만 아무 곳에도 연결되지 않은(부하 없는) 출력입니다. 그릴까요? (그림 / 생략)
  - 확신: low (계획서에 규칙 없음)
  - 가정한 답: 그림 — 분류가 고속이므로 §6.3(생략은 분류대로만)에 따라 생략하지 않고, 끝에 넷 이름표만 둔 짧은 선으로 표시.
- **Q7** `W<4:1>_<3:0>` — 중요 제어 16비트(W1_0..W4_3)를 "DFE 탭 계수" 기능 하나의 버스로 묶을까요, 탭별 4비트 버스 4개로 나눌까요? (16b 1개 / 4b×4)
  - 확신: low (계획서 해석)
  - 가정한 답: 16b 버스 1개 (W<4:1>_<3:0>) — §4.4 "기능별 버스 하나"를 "DFE 탭 계수" 기능으로 해석.

## 최상위 포트 분류

| 포트 | 분류 | 근거 |
|---|---|---|
| RX_INP | data | 사용자 지정 고속 입력 (구조 확인: CTLE1 입력쌍 게이트, 종단 R→공통모드, ESD) |
| RX_INN | data | 사용자 지정 고속 입력 (구조 확인: CTLE1 입력쌍 게이트, 종단 R→공통모드, ESD) |
| CLK_INP | clock | 클럭 입력: 구조 추적이 latch 클럭 스티어링/StrongARM 꼬리·리셋 게이트에 도달 (+ 이름 힌트 CLK) |
| CLK_INN | clock | 클럭 입력: 구조 추적이 latch 클럭 스티어링/StrongARM 꼬리·리셋 게이트에 도달 (+ 이름 힌트 CLK) |
| IREF | bias | 바이어스: diode-connected (mirror) node |
| DOUT_EVEN_P | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_EVEN/X_LATCH1/M1 (g->d)) |
| DOUT_EVEN_N | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_EVEN/X_LATCH1/M2 (g->d)) |
| DOUT_ODD_P | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_ODD/X_LATCH1/M1 (g->d)) |
| DOUT_ODD_N | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_ODD/X_LATCH1/M2 (g->d)) |
| C0 | general_control | 일반 제어: switch across X_CTLE1/SN-X_CTLE1/C0_MID (degeneration bank, not a summing branch); switch across X_CTLE1/SP-X_CTLE1/C0_MID (degeneration bank, not a summing branch) (이름-구조 불일치 → Q1) |
| C1 | general_control | 일반 제어: switch across X_CTLE1/SN-X_CTLE1/C1_MID (degeneration bank, not a summing branch); switch across X_CTLE1/SP-X_CTLE1/C1_MID (degeneration bank, not a summing branch) (이름-구조 불일치 → Q1) |
| C2 | general_control | 일반 제어: switch across X_CTLE1/SN-X_CTLE1/C2_MID (degeneration bank, not a summing branch); switch across X_CTLE1/SP-X_CTLE1/C2_MID (degeneration bank, not a summing branch) (이름-구조 불일치 → Q1) |
| R0 | general_control | 일반 제어: switch across X_CTLE1/SN-X_CTLE1/SP (degeneration bank, not a summing branch); switch across X_CTLE2/SN-X_CTLE2/SP (degeneration bank, not a summing branch) |
| R1 | general_control | 일반 제어: switch across X_CTLE1/SN-X_CTLE1/SP (degeneration bank, not a summing branch); switch across X_CTLE2/SN-X_CTLE2/SP (degeneration bank, not a summing branch) |
| R2 | general_control | 일반 제어: switch across X_CTLE1/SN-X_CTLE1/SP (degeneration bank, not a summing branch); switch across X_CTLE2/SN-X_CTLE2/SP (degeneration bank, not a summing branch) |
| G0 | general_control | 일반 제어: switch -> current of an extra pair with the SAME inputs CTLE2_N/CTLE2_P as the main pair (gain only) |
| G1 | general_control | 일반 제어: switch -> current of an extra pair with the SAME inputs CTLE2_N/CTLE2_P as the main pair (gain only) |
| G2 | general_control | 일반 제어: switch -> current of an extra pair with the SAME inputs CTLE2_N/CTLE2_P as the main pair (gain only) |

## 최상위 내부 넷 분류

| 넷 | 분류 | 근거 |
|---|---|---|
| CLKI | clock | 고속 클럭: CLK_INP/N 에서 추적 (via X_CLK/X_DIV_M/M1 (g->d)) |
| CLKIB | clock | 고속 클럭: CLK_INP/N 에서 추적 (via X_CLK/X_DIV_M/M2 (g->d)) |
| CLKQ | clock | 고속 클럭: CLK_INP/N 에서 추적 (via X_CLK/X_DIV_S/M1 (g->d)) |
| CLKQB | clock | 고속 클럭: CLK_INP/N 에서 추적 (via X_CLK/X_DIV_S/M2 (g->d)) |
| CTLE1_N | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_CTLE1/M2 (g->d)) |
| CTLE1_P | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_CTLE1/M1 (g->d)) |
| CTLE2_N | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_CTLE2/M2 (g->d)) |
| CTLE2_P | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_CTLE2/M1 (g->d)) |
| E_T2N | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_EVEN/X_DFF2/X_S/M2 (g->d)) |
| E_T2P | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_EVEN/X_DFF2/X_S/M1 (g->d)) |
| E_T3N | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_EVEN/X_DFF3/X_S/M2 (g->d)) |
| E_T3P | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_EVEN/X_DFF3/X_S/M1 (g->d)) |
| O_DOUTN | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via M_TIE_N (TG channel)) |
| O_DOUTP | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via M_TIE_P (TG channel)) |
| O_T2N | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_ODD/X_DFF2/X_S/M2 (g->d)) |
| O_T2P | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_ODD/X_DFF2/X_S/M1 (g->d)) |
| O_T3N | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_ODD/X_DFF3/X_S/M2 (g->d)) |
| O_T3P | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_DFE_ODD/X_DFF3/X_S/M1 (g->d)) |
| VBN | bias | 바이어스: diode-connected (mirror) node |
| VBP | bias | 바이어스: diode-connected (mirror) node |
| VCM_RX | bias | 바이어스: common-mode node of a termination |
| VGA_N | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_VGA/M_MAIN_N (g->d)) |
| VGA_P | data | 고속 데이터: RX_INP/RX_INN 에서 추적 (via X_VGA/M_MAIN_P (g->d)) |

## 블록 역할 (셀별)

| 셀 | 역할 | 기호 | 확신 | 근거 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| INV | inverter | triangle (CMOS buffer) | high | complementary PMOS/NMOS with common gate and drain |
| NAND2 | static_cmos_gate | box (cell name) | low | static CMOS gate (pull-up gate set = pull-down gate set): NOT in the dictionary |
| SR_LATCH | cross_coupled_gates | box (cell name) | low | two static CMOS gates cross-coupled (SR latch); NOT in the dictionary |
| STRONGARM_SLICER | composite | box (cell name) | high | hierarchical: 3 sub-blocks + 1 primitive unit(s); not itself a dictionary block |
| CML_LATCH | latch | box LATCH | high | input pair + cross-coupled pair, tails steered by complementary clocks |
| CML_DFF | dff | box DFF | high | two latches in series (master->slave) with complementary clocks |
| IDAC_4BIT | dac | variable current source (concept symbol in parent) | high | repeated (bias current source + series switch) units sharing one output |
| CTLE_STAGE | ctle | triangle "CTLE" | high | differential pair with separate sources joined by R/C (source degeneration) |
| VGA_STAGE | vga | triangle "VGA" | high | 5 pairs share outputs, all with the same inputs; extra pairs switched in |
| DFE_SUMMER | summer | Σ | high | 5 pairs with different inputs summed at shared outputs |
| HALF_RATE_DFE_SLICE | composite | box (cell name) | high | hierarchical: 5 sub-blocks + 0 primitive unit(s); not itself a dictionary block |
| CLK_DIST_TREE | composite | box (cell name) | high | hierarchical: 10 sub-blocks + 2 primitive unit(s); not itself a dictionary block |
| BIAS_GEN | bias | omit | high | diode/mirror devices only; no high-speed net |
| RX_AFE_TOP | composite | box (cell name) | high | hierarchical: 7 sub-blocks + 3 primitive unit(s); not itself a dictionary block |

## 셀 안의 기본 소자 묶음 (유닛)

- CML_LATCH: **latch** — MP_LP, MP_LN, M1, M2, M3, M4, M5, M6, MB — input pair + cross-coupled pair, tails steered by complementary clocks
- INV: **inverter** — MP, MN — complementary PMOS/NMOS with common gate and drain
- CLK_DIST_TREE: **termination** — R_TERM_P, R_TERM_N, C_VCM — termination: R from HS input to a common-mode node
- CLK_DIST_TREE: **cml_buffer** — MP_B1P, MP_B1N, MN_B1P, MN_B1N, MB_B1 — single differential pair, common tail, loads
- CTLE_STAGE: **ctle** — MP_LP, MP_LN, M_IND_P, M_IND_N, R_IND_P, R_IND_N, M1, M2, MBP, MBN, R_DEG_BASE, MR0, MR1, MR2, C_DEG_BASE, MC0P, MC0N, C0_CAP, MC1P, MC1N, C1_CAP, MC2P, MC2N, C2_CAP — differential pair with separate sources joined by R/C (source degeneration)
- VGA_STAGE: **vga** — MP_LP, MP_LN, M_MAIN_P, M_MAIN_N, MB_M, M_G0_P, M_G0_N, MS_G0, MB_G0, M_G1_P, M_G1_N, MS_G1, MB_G1, M_G2_P, M_G2_N, MS_G2, MB_G2, M_G3_P, M_G3_N, MS_G3, MB_G3 — 5 pairs share outputs, all with the same inputs; extra pairs switched in
- DFE_SUMMER: **summer** — MP_LP, MP_LN, M_MP, M_MN, MB_M, M_T1P, M_T1N, X_DAC1, M_T2P, M_T2N, X_DAC2, M_T3P, M_T3N, X_DAC3, M_T4P, M_T4N, X_DAC4 — 5 pairs with different inputs summed at shared outputs
- NAND2: **static_cmos_gate** — MP1, MP2, MN1, MN2 — static CMOS gate (pull-up gate set = pull-down gate set): NOT in the dictionary
- STRONGARM_SLICER: **strongarm** — M_TAIL, M1, M2, M3, M4, M5, M6, M7, M8, M9, M10, M11 — clocked tail, input pair, cross-coupled NMOS on input drains + cross-coupled PMOS, reset PMOS
- RX_AFE_TOP: **termination_esd** — R_RX_P, R_RX_N, C_RX_VCM, M_ESD_P1, M_ESD_N1, M_ESD_P2, M_ESD_N2 — termination + ESD: R from HS input to a common-mode node, MOS pad clamps with gate tied to own rail
- RX_AFE_TOP: **tg** — M_TIE_P, M_TIE_P2 — NMOS+PMOS between the same two nets, gates tied to rails
- RX_AFE_TOP: **tg** — M_TIE_N, M_TIE_N2 — NMOS+PMOS between the same two nets, gates tied to rails

## 참고: 데이터·클럭 겸용 셀

- CML_LATCH: 핀 DP, DN, QP, QN 는 DFE 경로에서는 데이터, CLK_DIST_TREE에서는 클럭 (선 굵기는 데이터 기준, 패널에 주석)
- INV: 핀 A, Y 는 DFE 경로에서는 데이터, CLK_DIST_TREE에서는 클럭 (선 굵기는 데이터 기준, 패널에 주석)

## 추적 중 멈춘 곳 (요약)

- M10: gate * reset/precharge (rail source, dynamic node * stop (×2)
- M5: gate * drain * has no load (tail / steering / stacked switch): stop (×12)
- M6: gate * drain * has no load (tail / steering / stacked switch): stop (×12)
- M7: gate * reset/precharge (rail source, dynamic node * stop (×2)
- M8: gate * reset/precharge (rail source, dynamic node * stop (×2)
- M9: gate * reset/precharge (rail source, dynamic node * stop (×2)
- MN2: gate * drain * has no load (tail / steering / stacked switch): stop (×4)
- M_TAIL: gate * drain * has no load (tail / steering / stacked switch): stop (×2)
- R_RX_N: common-mode node * stop (×1)
- R_RX_P: common-mode node * stop (×1)
- R_TERM_N: common-mode node * stop (×1)
- R_TERM_P: common-mode node * stop (×1)
````

   "assumed": "예 — 구조를 따름. C0..C2 스위치는 가변 R 묶음에 포함, C_DEG_BASE+C0_CAP+C1_CAP+C2_CAP은 고정 C 하나(합계 330f)로 그림. C0..C2는 일반 제어(그리지 않음).",
   "confidence": "low (이름-구조 불일치)"
  },
  {
   "id": "Q2",
   "item": "X_DIV_M / X_DIV_S in CLK_DIST_TREE",
   "question": "인스턴스 이름은 ÷2 분주기(DIV)이지만 구조상 귀환 루프가 없습니다 (master의 D 입력 = 자기 클럭 B1N/B1P, slave 출력은 어디로도 되돌아가지 않음). 구조대로 \"LATCH 2개 직렬\"로 그릴까요? (예 / ÷2로 그림 / netlist 오류)",
   "assumed": "예 — 구조대로 LATCH 박스 2개와 실제 연결을 그림. ÷2 기호는 쓰지 않음. netlist 오류 가능성을 보고서에 남김.",
   "confidence": "low (이름-구조 불일치, 사전의 ÷2 = latch 루프와 불일치)"
  },
  {
   "id": "Q3",
   "item": "M_TIE_P/M_TIE_P2, M_TIE_N/M_TIE_N2",
   "question": "전달 게이트(NMOS 게이트=VSS, PMOS 게이트=VDD)라 항상 꺼져 있습니다. 그러면 O_DOUTP/N(= X_DFE_EVEN의 탭1 귀환 입력 T1_FB)은 떠 있습니다. 의도된 것입니까? (의도됨 / 연결(항상 ON)이어야 함)",
   "assumed": "끊긴 것으로 처리(계획서 §3.2): TG는 열린 스위치 기호 + \"(off)\". O_DOUTP/N은 구동이 없지만 X_DFE_EVEN의 고속 핀(T1_FB)에 붙어 있어 고속 데이터로 그리고 이름표로 끝냄.",
   "confidence": "low (구조상 항상 OFF)"
  },
  {
   "id": "Q4",
   "item": "STRONGARM_SLICER: M11",
   "question": "STRONGARM_SLICER에 거울 짝이 없는 소자가 있습니다 (M11: PMOS, S=VDD, D=DP, G=DN; 반대쪽 D=DN, G=DP 소자 없음). 차동 회로가 비대칭이 됩니다. 의도된 것입니까? (의도됨 / netlist 오류)",
   "assumed": "그대로 그림 (정확성 > 대칭, 계획서 §5.2-7). M11은 연결된 노드(DP) 쪽 바깥에 두고, 게이트(DN)는 이름표로 연결, \"비대칭\" 표시.",
   "confidence": "low (대칭 위반이 netlist에 있음)"
  },
  {
   "id": "Q5",
   "item": "NAND2 (static_cmos_gate), SR_LATCH (cross_coupled_gates)",
   "question": "블록 역할 사전에 없는 구조입니다 (정적 CMOS 게이트, 교차 결합 게이트 = SR latch). 박스(셀 이름)로 그릴까요? (예 / 역할 지정)",
   "assumed": "예 — 사전 규칙대로 박스(셀 이름) + 보이는 핀. 유닛 레벨은 소자 그대로.",
   "confidence": "low (사전에 없음)"
  },
  {
   "id": "Q6",

   "question": "추적상 고속 넷이지만 아무 곳에도 연결되지 않은(부하 없는) 출력입니다. 그릴까요? (그림 / 생략)",
   "assumed": "그림 — 분류가 고속이므로 §6.3(생략은 분류대로만)에 따라 생략하지 않고, 끝에 넷 이름표만 둔 짧은 선으로 표시.",
   "confidence": "low (계획서에 규칙 없음)"
  },
  {

  }
